APPLICATION INFORMATION ...
The HRProprietary Engine provides a coherent application environment. It is bundled with an application rather than installed beside one.

FILE INFORMATION ...
v3.9.7.
This plain text file contains descriptions of all the native error messages contained in or produced by the HRConvert2 Diablo engine.
These descriptions should give administrators a starting point for debugging problems with HRConvert2 installations.
Each section in this file contains information about a different error message. The first one is an example to demonstrate the structure of this file.
Details about each section are below.

HARDWARE REQUIREMENTS ...
This application requires at least a Raspberry Pi Model B+ or greater.
This application will run on just about any x86 or x64 computer.

DEPENDENCY REQUIREMENTS ...
What the Engine itself needs is Resources/Engine/depends.php, & --setup installs it with the application's own half. Run  php <entry point> -v  to see what an installation has.

ADDITIONAL INFORMATION ...
HRConvert2 logs information about its operational activity to the Logs subdirectory of the data location by default.
Root logs to the Engine subdirectory beside it, & repeats its warnings & errors on the terminal when run there by hand.
A command line account other than root logs to a folder of its own in the temporary directory.
The default logging directory can be adjusted by modifying the --Log Storage Directory-- section of the config.php file.
Log entries that represent normal, expected behavior begin with 'Op-Act,' to signify "Operational Activity".
Log entries that represent abnormal behavior begin with 'WARNING!!!' or 'ERROR!!!'.
If a log file does not exist, HRConvert2 will attempt to create one.
If an attempt to create a log file or write log entries fails for any reason, HRConvert2 will output errors directly to the page instead.
If a log file exists, was created on the current date, and is smaller than the "Maximum Log File Size" in config.php, HRConvert2 will append log entries to it.
Once the "Maximum Log File Size" specified in config.php has been reached, HRConvert2 will create a new log file with a numerically incremented file name.
Actual maximum log file size will vary slightly from the "Maximum Log File Size" because it must be exceeded before HRConvert2 will replace it.
Typically the "Maximum Log File Size" is only exceeded by a small number of bytes.
Time will be omitted from logs in situations where time has not been initialized yet.
The application name will be replaced with 'HRConvert2' in errors in situations where the config.php file has not been initialized yet.
The log number will be replaced with '###' in the event that the error number supplied by the calling code was invalid.
If "Enhanced Logging Verbosity" is enabled in config.php, all significant core operations will produce a log entry.
If "Enhanced Logging Verbosity" is disabled in config.php, only warnings and errors during core operations will produce a log entry.
As of v3.8.9 every entry carries a sequence number immediately after its tier label.
An error therefore reads  ERROR!!! 00042, <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-####, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: message
The number counts entries rather than seconds and starts at zero when the process starts.
A gap in that sequence is the only evidence that an entry was written and never arrived.
Two entries stamped with the same second are otherwise impossible to order.
Every example below omits the sequence number for readability. The number is present in a real log and is not part of the error itself.
Log files are also named for the role that writes them, as HRConvert2_Web, HRConvert2_Manager or HRConvert2_Cli.
An error raised by a web request is therefore in the Web log rather than in a single shared file.
See Documentation/ABOUT_LOGGING.txt for the whole of this.
As of v3.8.5 every conversion pipeline is a detachable component beneath /Resources/Pipelines/ rather than code inside convertCore.php.
No error number changed value when those pipelines moved. Only the FILE section of each affected entry was updated to name its new location.
An operator who read a number in an older release reads exactly the same number today.
Every FILE section below was derived by scanning the code for the call that raises each number rather than by assuming which pipeline owns a block.
A number raised from more than one file names every file that raises it.
The 2000 block is raised by /Resources/Pipelines/Shared/libreOffice.php, which serves the Document pipeline and the OCR pipeline alike.
An operator seeing one of those numbers may have been running either one. The log line above it names which.
The Pipeline Core reserves 34000 through 34019. It became an Engine core at v3.9.9 & none of its numbers changed.
The 32000 and 33000 blocks were already taken by Setup Core and Dependency Core.
The Identify pipeline reserves 37000 through 37004. It is the first pipeline to number its own errors.
The Archiver & Scan pipelines took archiving & scanning for a user out of convertCore.php at v4.1.6, & every number came with them.
The Archiver raises the 4000 block & 13012, & 13006 through 13010 with the Archive pipeline. The Scan pipeline raises the 16000,
17000, 19000 & 20000 blocks. No number changed value.
The archiveExtraction.php shared module reserves 39000 through 39019 & the Extractor pipeline 39100 through 39119, from v4.1.7.
The Archive pipeline raises 39000 through 39003 too, because it extracts an archive through that module.
Renaming in convertCore.php reserves 23100 through 23119, the Metadata pipeline 41000 through 41019 & the PDF pipeline 42000 through 42019.
The Authentication Core reserves 43000 through 43019 & the Email Core 43100 through 43119, from v4.2.0.
The Data pipeline reserves 44000 through 44019, the LaTeX pipeline 45000 through 45019, the Trace pipeline 46000 through 46019,
the Email pipeline 47000 through 47019 & the Markup pipeline 48000 through 48019, from v4.2.5.
The Graph pipeline reserves 49000 through 49019, from v4.2.6.
The Model pipeline raises 9000 through 9008, from v4.3.2, where it raised 9000 through 9004.

<3 Open-Source

--About An HTTP Status Code, Which Is Not One Of Ours--

A numbered error in this file is one HRConvert2 raised deliberately.
A status code is what the web server said when HRConvert2 raised nothing at all.
They are different problems & the difference tells you where to look.

  A numbered error means the application ran & decided something was wrong.
  A status code usually means the application did not get far enough to decide anything.

500, INTERNAL SERVER ERROR.
PHP stopped. Nothing in this file will explain it, because nothing here was reached.
Look in the web server error log rather than in the HRConvert2 log.
  /var/log/apache2/error.log on Debian & Ubuntu.
  docker logs <container> for a container.
The line you want names a file & a line number & begins with PHP Fatal error.
  A call to an undefined function means a component did not load, or was called before it
  loaded. Run  php convertCore.php -v  & read which components report FAILED.
  A permission denied on a path means the web server user cannot reach it. Run  -fp  as
  root, which corrects ownership on every path this application manages.
  An undefined variable that is fatal usually means a configuration this release requires
  is missing. Compare $ConfigVersion in config.php against $RequiredConfigVersion.
  Anything mentioning memory_limit or max_execution_time is PHP refusing to continue rather
  than HRConvert2 failing. The -fp argument writes a drop in that raises both.

404, NOT FOUND.
The web server did not find the file, so HRConvert2 was never involved.
  Check the address. The application lives at /HRProprietary/HRConvert2/convertCore.php &
  not at the directory above it.
  A 404 on a file that exists on disk is an Apache configuration problem rather than a
  missing file. Run  -v  & read the Apache configuration line.
  A 404 for everything under the data location is CORRECT & is what protects it.
  The Live exposure line in  -v  reports PROTECTED when that is working.

403, FORBIDDEN.
The web server found the file & refused to serve it.
  Almost always ownership. Run  -fp  as root.
  A 403 on the data location is the protection working, exactly as a 404 there is.

502 or 504, BAD GATEWAY or GATEWAY TIMEOUT.
A reverse proxy in front of this application gave up waiting.
  A long conversion outlives a default proxy timeout. Raise it on the proxy rather than
  here, because HRConvert2 has no way to make the proxy wait.
  A detached conversion is supervised after the reply is sent, so the proxy timing out does
  not stop the work. The file appears when the worker finishes.

413, PAYLOAD TOO LARGE.
The upload was refused before HRConvert2 saw it.
  This is upload_max_filesize & post_max_size in PHP, & LimitRequestBody in Apache.
  The -fp argument writes the PHP side. The Apache side is yours.

A BLANK PAGE WITH NO STATUS CODE AT ALL.
PHP produced output & then died, or produced nothing & exited zero.
  Check the web server error log first, exactly as for a 500.
  A blank page after an upload is usually a warning printed into an AJAX reply. The
  interface reads that reply as a filename & shows nothing. Search the HRConvert2 log for a
  WARNING at the same second.

WHAT TO GATHER BEFORE ASKING FOR HELP.
  php convertCore.php -v            every component, dependency & subsystem, with versions.
  The last fifty lines of the HRConvert2 log for the role that failed.
  The web server error log for the same minute.
  Whether the same operation works from the command line, which separates a web server
  problem from an application problem in one step.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  The exact error response produced by the application.
  Text wrapped in angle brackets < > denote encoded information.
  <TIMESTAMP> will be replaced with an abbreviated date.
  <SESSION_IDENTIFIER> will be replaced with a short but unique number to identify related log events.
  <APPLICATION_NAME> will be replaced with whatever the --Application Name String-- is set to in config.php.
  <FILE_PATH> will be replaced with an absolute file path.
  <FILE_NAME> will be replaced with a file name.
  <ADDITIONAL_DATA> will be replaced with the result of a system call or standard output from a called dependency.
  <FILE_TYPE> will be replaced with a human readable one word string describing the file such as 'Video' or 'Document'.
  <FILE_EXTENSION> will be replaced with a file extension.

FILE
  The file path pointing to the file which contains this error message.
  File is relative to the root HRConvert2 installation directory.
  <SELECTED_LANGUAGE> will be replaced with the ISO 639-1 code that represents the language that was used during the event.
  <SELECTED_GUI> will be replaced with a text string that represents the GUI that was used during the event.
  <SELECTED_COLOR> will be replaced with a text string that represents the color that was used during the event.

FATAL
  Whether or not the occurrence of this error will halt execution of the application.
  Will either be YES or NO.

LOGGED
  Whether or not the occurrence of this error will be written to a log file in the --Log Storage Directory-- section of the config.php file.
  Will either be YES or NO.

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A brief and concise description of the error message as well as possible causes & solutions.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
~~END OF EXAMPLE~~


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-0, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not process the HRConvert2 Configuration file (config.php)!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-0, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: No configuration file & no Setup Core to write one!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-0, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: No configuration file & no Engine to write one!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-0, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: No configuration file & no Environment Core to write one! <ADDITIONAL_DATA>

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Resources/config.php does not exist, or the web server account cannot reach it.
  This is raised before the logging environment exists, so it reaches the page & the server
  error log rather than the HRConvert2 log.
  Confirm Resources/config.php exists & is readable by the web server account.
  Restore it from a backup beside it, named config.php.hrconvert2-<DATE>.bak, if one exists.
  Otherwise write one from the template, with every setting at its default.
    sudo php convertCore.php --config --repair
  A file that exists & will not parse is a PHP fatal error rather than this one, & the web
  server error log names its line.
  The second, third & fourth wordings come from that repair command. It writes the file with the
  Setup Core, the Engine & the Engine's Environment Core, & one of them is missing. Restore it from
  the release this core came from. The fourth is from v4.3.1, & what follows it says whether the
  Environment Core is missing, reports no version, reports another version or could not be loaded.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! HRConvert2-2: This file cannot process your request! Please submit your file to convertCore.php instead!

FILE
  /Languages/<SELECTED_LANGUAGE>/header.php
  /Languages/<SELECTED_LANGUAGE>/convertGui1.php
  /Languages/<SELECTED_LANGUAGE>/convertGui2.php
  /Languages/<SELECTED_LANGUAGE>/footer.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  NO

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A 3rd party application is improperly attempting to submit data to the API.
  The application is being fuzzed or scanned for vulnerabilities.
  This is the only core error message that is translated to the language used during the request.
  Because this error takes place before the logging environment is initialized, this error will not be logged.
  Because this error takes place before the time environment is initialized, this error will not contain a timestamp.
  Because this error takes place before the configuration file has been initialized, the Application Name configuration entry will not be used.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-3, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not set the execution timer!

FILE
  convertCore.php
  app.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  set_time_limit was refused, which happens when PHP is running in safe mode or when a
  hosting environment forbids it.
  Nothing this application does is safe without a limit, because a conversion that never
  finishes holds a worker slot until the process is killed.
  Check disable_functions in php.ini for set_time_limit.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-4, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not verify timezone!

FILE
  convertCore.php
  app.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  PHP's timezone is not one this PHP recognizes. It comes from date.timezone in php.ini, or
  from the system default when that is unset, & config.php has no setting for it.
  Every log entry, every session directory & every deletion threshold is derived from the
  time, so an unknown timezone makes all three untrustworthy rather than merely wrong.
  Set date.timezone in php.ini to a name PHP recognizes, such as America/New_York.
  A list of names is available from timedatectl list-timezones.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-5, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not verify installation!

FILE
  convertCore.php
  app.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  One of the checks verifyInstallation performs did not pass & the reason was reported by
  whichever check failed rather than by this line.
  Read the entry immediately above this one, which names the actual cause.
  On the command line, an account other than root whose per user secret folder was refused.
  The warning above names the folder, which another account created first or made a link.
  Delete it as root & run the command again.
  Run -v, which performs the same checks & reports every one of them rather than stopping
  at the first failure.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-9, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not verify logging environment!

FILE
  convertCore.php
  app.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  NO

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The path to the Logs directory specified in the --Log Storage Directory-- section of the config.php file does not exist or cannot be accessed.
  Logs in the data location is tried when that one cannot be made, & this means neither held a logfile.
  From v4.1.1 a WARNING above this error names each folder that could not be made, & the logfile that could not be created.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the directory where Logs are being stored.
  This may be the result of incorrect user or group associations for the www-data user or group.
  The www-data:www-data user:group needs to have read & write access to the Logs directory.
  The device where data is stored has an insufficient amount of storage space available.
  Root never reaches this through the Logs directory. Root logs to Engine in the data location, & to
  standard error when its logfile there cannot be opened, so as root this means standard error itself
  could not be opened. A WARNING above it says why the logfile in Engine could not be, unless Engine
  does not exist yet, as before -fp has run after an upgrade.
  Because this error takes place before the logging environment is initialized, this error will not be logged.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-15, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not verify color scheme! Color set to <SELECTED_COLOR>!

FILE
  /convertCore.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A color scheme could not be established for this session.
  NOTE. An invalid color does NOT cause this error. verifyColors() falls back to blue.
  A color named in the --Supported Colors-- config entry that HRConvert2 does not implement
  is ignored & the fallback is used instead.
  This error fires only when even the fallback could not be resolved.
  The styleCore.php file for the selected GUI is missing, unreadable or corrupt.
  The styleCore.php file does not define every button color variable the core expects.
  The www-data:www-data user:group needs read access to the UI subdirectory.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the UI subdirectory.
  Consult the WARNING entries immediately preceding this error for the color that failed.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-16, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not verify language! Language set to <SELECTED_LANGUAGE>!

FILE
  /convertCore.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A language pack could not be established for this session.
  NOTE. A missing or invalid language does NOT cause this error on its own.
  verifyLanguage() falls back to the --Default Language-- & then to English.
  This error fires only when the fallback language pack ALSO could not be verified.
  The UI/<SELECTED_GUI>/Languages/ directory does not exist or cannot be read.
  The UI/<SELECTED_GUI>/Languages/<SELECTED_LANGUAGE>/languageStrings.php file is missing.
  The UI/<SELECTED_GUI>/Languages/<SELECTED_LANGUAGE>/flag.png file is missing.
  A language pack requires BOTH files. A pack missing either one is not loaded.
  The www-data:www-data user:group needs read access to the Languages subdirectory.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the Languages subdirectory.
  Consult the WARNING entries immediately preceding this error for the packs that failed.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-17, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not display GUI!

FILE
  /convertCore.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The GUI was assembled but did not complete.
  This error has two distinct causes & the WARNING entries above it identify which.
  FIRST CAUSE. No language pack actually loaded. A pack sets $LanguageStringsLoaded itself,
  so a languageStrings.php that is present but does not run to completion, with no baseline
  pack under it, produces this. A pack reporting the wrong version does NOT. That is a
  warning, & the baseline pack loaded underneath supplies any string the pack lacks.
  A pack that is missing altogether is error 16 rather than this one.
  SECOND CAUSE. One of the four interface files did not set its display flag.
  header.php, convertGui1.php or convertGui2.php, & footer.php must each report success.
  A file that is present but did not execute to completion produces this.
  The www-data:www-data user:group needs read access to the UI subdirectory.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the UI subdirectory.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-18, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Upload Failed!

FILE
  convertCore.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The path to the Data directory specified in the --Data Storage Directory-- section of the config.php file does not exist or cannot be accessed.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the directory where Data is stored.
  This may be the result of incorrect user or group associations for the www-data user or group.
  The web server account needs read & write access to Sessions, Cache, Logs & Sockets in the data location, which -fp gives it. The data location itself is root's.
  The device where data is stored has an insufficient amount of storage space available.
  The disk holding the session has --Storage Reserve-- megabytes free or less, or would have once the upload
  was written, so the listener refused it. A WARNING!!! above this error says so, & ERROR 31026 says when the
  disk entered the reserve. Free space on that disk, & the next upload is accepted.
  The request contained character types or encoded data that are not supported.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-20, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Archive Failed!

FILE
  convertCore.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The user submitted a request to archive a file that does not exist.
  The user submitted a request that was incorrect, malformed, or corrupt.

  The path to the Data directory specified in the --Data Storage Directory-- section of the config.php file does not exist or cannot be accessed.
  The web server account needs read access to the installation, & read & write access to DATA in the hosted location.
  The web server account needs read & write access to Sessions, Cache, Logs & Sockets in the data location, which -fp gives it. The data location itself is root's.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the directory where the application is installed.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the directory where Data is stored.
  This may be the result of incorrect user or group associations for the www-data user or group.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-21, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Conversion Failed!

FILE
  convertCore.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A conversion produced nothing, so the request was ended.
  The entry above this one says why, under the number the pipeline raised, such as 7001 for a document, & its description says what to check.
  A converter that failed on the file, one killed for going over the memory ceiling it was given, a file the session no longer holds because it outlived --File Deletion Age Threshold--, & a file that was never uploaded are the usual causes.
  Sessions are kept in Sessions in the data location, which only the web server account may write. -fp gives each managed location its owner & mode. See Documentation/ABOUT_MANAGED_LOCATIONS.txt.
  A data location with no space left fails every conversion this way.
  Until v4.0.6 this described a layout no release has used since v4.0.1, in which the web server account owned the whole data location.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-26, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not clean the build environment!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/engine.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  HRConvert2 was asked to remove the build environment or the development documentation and could not.
  This work is controlled by --Delete Build Environment-- and --Delete Development Documentation-- in config.php.
  Both settings default to leaving those files in place, so this error only occurs when one has been enabled.
  The removal is done by root, from the -fp argument, & an update runs -fp as it finishes.
  Until v4.0.1 every web request tried it as the web server user, which can no longer write to the installation.
  Root only removes a file from a folder it trusts. Every folder from / down must belong to root & be writable by nobody else.
  A WARNING entry just above this error names the file that was left & the folder root does not trust.
  A folder such as /var/www/html that belongs to the web server user is the usual cause. -fp does not change it, because it is not this application's.
  ERROR 26000 & ERROR 26001 say which of the two removals failed.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-30, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The secret file at <PATH> could not be removed by <USER>. Delete it as root & run the command again.

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A standard user's own secret file is unusable & could not be deleted so a new one could be written.
  A command line user who is neither root nor the web server user keeps a secret of its own, in a
  folder of its own in the temporary directory, & this is that file. -fp does not manage it.
  The file was created there by a different account, or it is on a read only mount.
  Delete the file named in the message as root & run the command again.
  Only a standard user reaches this. Root replaces the install secret itself, & the web server
  user never removes or creates one. ERROR 31021 & ERROR 31020 cover the install secret.
  Until v4.0.1 this message sent the user to -fp, which never touched this file.
  This error is not fatal on its own. Without a secret the installation does not verify, so
  ERROR 5 follows & that user's command does not run until the file is removed.

FILE
  convertCore.php

  YES

LOGGED

ERROR DESCRIPTION

  No Identify pipeline could be reached. ERROR 34006 precedes this one.
  The device where data is stored has an insufficient amount of storage space available.
  A missing tool does not cause this error. The file is still identified by its size, its date & its hashes, & a note names the tool.
  Neither does a refused sandbox. ERROR 37004 reports that, & every file is still hashed.
  Identifying changes no file, so nothing a user holds is lost when this error fires.
  Until v4.1.2 this number was unused.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-502, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: ClamAV is missing, too old, or unusable, so nothing was scanned!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/ClamAV/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  clamscan could not be located on the server.
  clamscan was located but reported a version older than the minimum Resources/Contract/depends.php gives it.
  clamscan was located but did not report a version that could be read at all.
  Nothing in the request was scanned. No clean result is implied by this error & none should be inferred.
  The scan reports failure rather than a clean result, because an absent scanner produces the
  same silence a clean scan produces & the two must never be confused.
  The caller answers this with a fatal 'Could not perform a virus scan!' error, so a server with
  --Virus Scanning-- enabled & no usable scanner refuses to process files rather than processing
  them unscanned.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-503, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The <LOCATION> directory is returning a server error, so no download & no share link can work. The usual cause is a directive in <LOCATION>/.htaccess that this directory's AllowOverride level does not permit; the web server error log names it. Delete that file to restore service, then apply the rules in the server configuration instead.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/environmentCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The DATA exposure check fetched its canary & the web server answered with a 5xx.
  THIS IS AN OUTAGE, NOT AN EXPOSURE. Every download & every share link is failing while
  this is true, because nothing in that tree is being served at all.
  The usual cause is a directive in DATA/.htaccess that the AllowOverride level configured
  for that directory does not permit. Apache REFUSES such a request rather than ignoring the
  directive, & an <IfModule> guard does not prevent it, because <IfModule> tests whether a
  module is loaded rather than whether a directive is allowed.
  Only Header & RemoveHandler are legal under AllowOverride FileInfo. Options & php_flag
  require AllowOverride Options & return this error wherever that is not granted, which is
  why the file this application writes contains neither.
  The web server error log names the offending line. Deleting DATA/.htaccess restores service
  immediately. Apply the rules in the server configuration instead, where no override level
  applies. See Documentation/ABOUT_DATA_DIRECTORY_PROTECTION.txt.
  A hand edited DATA/.htaccess is the other common cause.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-504, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The Apache configuration <APPLICATION_NAME> wrote did not pass apachectl configtest & was withdrawn. The test reported: <ADDITIONAL_DATA>

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The -fp argument wrote the DATA rules into the Apache configuration, ran apachectl
  configtest, & the test failed for a reason that was NOT already present before the write.
  THE FILE WAS WITHDRAWN & WHAT WAS THERE BEFORE IS BACK. A file this run created is removed,
  a file it replaced is written back exactly as it was, & the conf-enabled link is removed only
  when this run made it. Nothing was reloaded, so the server is still running the configuration
  it was running before this ran. configtest is then run once more, & a WARNING follows this
  error if the configuration still does not parse.
  Until v4.0.1 a replaced file was deleted rather than put back.
  A configuration that does not parse takes the whole server down on its next restart, which
  is a far worse outcome than the exposure these rules close, so the rules are never left in
  place unless they are known to parse.
  The apachectl output is quoted in the error & names the offending line.
  A directive whose module is not loaded is a syntax error, so every directive in the file
  that needs a module is guarded by it. One that is not, added by hand, is the likeliest cause.
  A host whose configuration ALREADY did not parse before this ran is reported differently &
  the HRConvert2 file is KEPT, because that fault belongs to the administrator rather than to
  this application.
  The DATA/.htaccess remains as the fallback. Run the -fp argument again after correcting the
  cause & read the DATA exposure line it prints to confirm the tree is protected.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-2001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed LibreOffice version is missing, unidentifiable, or too old!

FILE
  /Resources/Pipelines/Shared/libreOffice.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  LibreOffice is the engine behind every document, spreadsheet, presentation & XPS conversion.
  The Document Conversion Engine passes work to it, so no document conversion can succeed without it.
  LibreOffice may not be installed, or may not be reachable on the system path used by the web server user.
  The installed LibreOffice may be older than the minimum Resources/Contract/depends.php gives it.
  This minimum exists for format support & conversion reliability & is not a security control.
  LibreOffice changed versioning schemes in 2024, moving from version 7.6 directly to version 24.2.
  The new scheme is year.month, so a major of 24 or higher is newer than a major of 7.
  HRConvert2 compares these values numerically, so both schemes are ranked correctly.
  Some distributions ship only the soffice binary, & HRConvert2 tries that name when libreoffice is absent.
  HRConvert2 refuses any build it cannot positively identify, because an unknown build cannot be cleared.
  Check the installed version by running 'libreoffice --version' as the web server user.
  Nothing is started. Each conversion runs LibreOffice as its own sandboxed process.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  LibreOffice is installed but its binary is outside every directory HRConvert2 searches.

  The web server user cannot execute the binary due to file permission levels.
  Install LibreOffice, or confirm that soffice appears in the output of the -v argument.
  This error is not fatal but the file that causes it will be skipped.

  /Resources/Pipelines/Shared/libreOffice.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The requested output format contained no character that could be used as a format name.
  The user submitted a request that was incorrect, malformed, or corrupt.
  A 3rd party application is improperly attempting to submit data to the API.
  The application is being fuzzed or scanned for vulnerabilities.
  The format is filtered to letters, numbers, a dot, a dash, an underscore & a colon before use.
  A colon is permitted because LibreOffice accepts a filter name appended to a format.
  This error is not fatal but the file that causes it will be skipped.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-2004, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A document conversion was refused because no sandbox could be built!

FILE
  /Resources/Pipelines/Shared/libreOffice.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, & a document is never converted without it.
  A document is the single most dangerous input this application accepts.
  LibreOffice will resolve a linked image, an INCLUDEPICTURE field & an external relationship
  target by fetching the URL, which turns a document upload into a request made by this server.
  It also draws an image a document names by a relative or an absolute path on this server, &
  nothing passed to it stops that. The sandbox removes the network namespace & shows LibreOffice
  none of this server's files but the document, which is what stops both.
  From v4.2.7 the libreoffice sandbox profile sets RequireSandbox, so --Require Sandbox-- &
  --Require Sandbox On Docker-- do not decide this. Until then it occurred only where the one that
  applies was TRUE, & setting it to FALSE ran documents unprotected.
  A WARNING!!! above it, from the sandbox, names the profile. Install bubblewrap, or repair it.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  as root & read the Sandbox line.
  This error is not fatal but the file that causes it will be skipped.
  From v4.2.8 the Document pipeline does not try a refused conversion again, so this is written once & 7001 does not follow it.
  Until then it was written once for each attempt --File Operation Retry Count-- allows, & 7001 followed.
  An XPS conversion that cannot be sandboxed raises 2005 instead.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-2005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: An XPS conversion was refused because no sandbox could be built!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Document/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Bubblewrap is missing or non functional & sandboxing is required by config.php.
  Outside a container this error only occurs if --Require Sandbox-- is set to TRUE in config.php.
  In a container it only occurs if --Require Sandbox On Docker-- is set to TRUE, & --Require Sandbox-- is not read.
  An XPS or OXPS file is converted by xpstopdf, which parses a document format from an untrusted upload.
  The sandbox removes the network namespace, which is what stops a linked resource being fetched.
  This error was previously reported as 2004 & was separated so the two conversions can be told apart.
  ERROR 2004 is the same condition raised for every other document conversion.
  Install bubblewrap, or set the relevant setting to FALSE to run conversions unprotected.
  This error is not fatal but the file that causes it will be skipped.
  From v4.2.8 the Document pipeline does not try a refused conversion again, so this is written once & 7001 does not follow it.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-2006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A document conversion was refused because no private LibreOffice profile could be made!

FILE
  /Resources/Pipelines/Shared/libreOffice.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  No sandbox could be built, running without one is permitted, & the private folder LibreOffice is
  given for its profile on that path could not be made in the temporary directory.
  A conversion without a sandbox is never run on a shared profile path, because any account on the
  host could make that folder first & a LibreOffice profile can carry macros. That document is
  refused & nothing else changes.
  The temporary directory, or the one TMPDIR names, may be missing, full, or not writable by the
  account running the conversion.
  Building the sandbox removes the need for the folder entirely. Run -v, which reports it.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-2007, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A document conversion was refused, because LibreOffice could not be given a copy of <FILE_NAME> & would have replaced <FILE_NAME>!

FILE
  /Resources/Pipelines/Shared/libreOffice.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  LibreOffice names what it writes after what it reads, & replaces a file that already has that name.
  It is normally given a copy of the document named after the output, in a folder of its own beside
  the document, so the only name it writes is the one the output was given. That copy could not be
  made, & a file in the session already holds the name LibreOffice would write from the original.
  The document is refused rather than converted over that file. Nothing else changes.
  The temporary folder for the session may be full or not writable by the web server account.
  Run -fp, which repairs the data location.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

FILE
  /Resources/Pipelines/Archiver/pipeline.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A file selected to be archived is scanned first when --Virus Scanning-- is enabled in config.php, & this is what that scan said.
  The file was found infected, so it was not archived & the request ended here.
  The file is refused & left where it was. Whether an infected file is deleted is the application's policy, applied when a file
  lands, & a pipeline never decides it. See --Virus Scanning-- & --User Virus Scanning-- in config.php.
  Until v4.1.6 a scan that failed or found a virus was reported by the core first, & this followed it. The Archiver pipeline asks
  runVirusScan() itself now, so this is the entry that reports it.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-4005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: An iso image holds one file at a time, so a selection of files cannot be archived into one!

FILE
  /Resources/Pipelines/Archiver/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A selection of more than one file was sent to be archived & the requested output was an iso
  image. Nothing was written & no image was produced.
  Every other archiver offered here adds a file to an archive that already exists, which is how
  a selection of files becomes one archive. Mkisofs replaces the image it is given instead, so
  the second file would leave an image holding only the last one.
  Archive the selection as zip, 7z, tar or rar, or archive one file at a time as iso.
  A single file to iso is unaffected & is not this error.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-4006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: No archiver here writes <FORMAT>, so nothing was archived!

FILE
  /Resources/Pipelines/Archiver/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A selection of files was sent to be archived in a format the Archiver pipeline cannot make, such as a bootable disk image.
  Nothing was written. The core offers only the formats both config.php & the Archiver pipeline name, so a page this server
  drew does not send one, & a request that does was made by hand or by a page drawn before the formats changed.
  A bootable disk image is made by the Archive pipeline, from an archive that already holds a bootloader. See
  ABOUT_CREATING_BOOTABLE_ISO_IMAGES.txt. Until v4.1.6 every bootable format was offered for archiving a file, & each failed
  with error 4004 instead.
  ERROR 20 follows this one, because nothing was archived.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-5006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The conversion <FILE_EXTENSION> to <FILE_EXTENSION> is not supported!

FILE
  /convertCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  No conversion family's lists in config.php hold both the file's extension & the extension asked for.
  A menu sends the family it offers, from v4.2.8, & a request naming a family that does not convert the pair is warned of first.
  Until v4.2.8 the Image, Drawing & archive menus & the upload page were drawn from lists the converter did not read, so a file
  such as an xcf or a gz was offered a conversion that ended here.
  From v4.3.0 the Document family converts a pair only where one of its kinds, documents, spreadsheets, presentations or XPS,
  lists both, as its menus always offered. xlsx to docx, or xps to anything but pdf, ends here where no other family converts it.
  LibreOffice made nothing of either, & xpstopdf wrote a PDF under the name asked for. The Stream family reads only what
  --Supported Stream Formats-- lists, so mp3 to mp4, which no other family converts, ends here, where the stream inspector
  refused it with 21001.
  Other errors preceding this error will help narrow down the cause of this error.

ERROR MESSAGE

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Document/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

  LibreOffice, which converts every document, could not be verified.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-7001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The document converter produced no file after <ADDITIONAL_DATA> attempt(s)!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Document/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  LibreOffice ran & left no file. From v4.3.0 it is asked once, because LibreOffice that runs to its end & writes nothing, as it
  does for a pair it has no filter for, would write nothing again. Only one something outside it stopped, as a memory ceiling or a
  supervisor stops it with KILL or TERM, is asked again, as many times as --File Operation Retry Count-- allows. One that crashed
  is not, from v4.3.2, because it would crash again on the same document. Until v4.3.0 every attempt was made, & each waited a
  second longer than the last.
  From v4.3.0 the Document family no longer claims a pair across its kinds, so docx to pptx is Pandoc's & no longer ends here.
  Until v4.0.6 this read The document converter timed out! No time limit ever caused it, & one more attempt was made after it.
  With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the entry immediately above this one carries what LibreOffice printed, which usually names the cause.
  LibreOffice converts every document but an XPS file, which xpstopdf converts.
  A document LibreOffice cannot open & a format it does not write are the usual causes.
  From v4.2.8 a conversion LibreOffice was never run for, because it was refused, is not tried again & does not raise this. The
  refusal's own error, such as 2004, says why.
  From v4.0.6 a LibreOffice its ceiling killed is named in a warning above this, by the shared module.
  A converter its memory ceiling killed ends the same way. With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the Conversion Limit entry names that ceiling, & --Maximum Per Conversion Resources-- in config.php sets the most each type may be given.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-8000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The image converter produced no file after <ADDITIONAL_DATA> attempt(s)!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Image/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  ImageMagick ran & left no file. From v4.3.1 it is asked again only where a signal stopped it,
  as many times as --File Operation Retry Count-- allows. Until then it was asked that many times whatever happened.
  Until v4.0.6 this read The image converter timed out! No time limit ever caused it, & one more attempt was made after it.
  With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the entry immediately above this one carries what ImageMagick printed, which usually names the cause.
  A file ImageMagick cannot read, a format this build does not write & a coder the policy the pipeline carries refuses are the usual causes.
  From v4.2.8 ImageMagick is told the format it writes, so a format it has no encoder for ends here. avif & heif are written only where
  libheif has an encoder for them, & until v4.2.8 a server without one wrote a png under the name asked for.
  From v4.3.1 ImageMagick never runs without a sandbox, & error 8002 says where one could not be built. Until then a conversion that ran
  with no sandbox was under the host's ImageMagick policy too, & from v4.3.0 build-imagemagick-v7.sh installs ImageMagick's secure policy,
  which reads no PDF, & no image over 8000 pixels wide or high or 32 frames long, so each of those ended here.
  A converter its memory ceiling killed ends the same way. With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the Conversion Limit entry names that ceiling, & --Maximum Per Conversion Resources-- in config.php sets the most each type may be given.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-8001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed ImageMagick version is missing, unidentifiable, or too old!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Image/pipeline.php
  /Resources/Pipelines/OCR/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  ImageMagick is the engine behind every raster image, canvas adjustment, & vector rasterization conversion.
  The Image Conversion Engine passes work to it, so no image conversion can succeed without it.
  ImageMagick may not be installed, or may not be reachable on the system path used by the web server user.
  The installed ImageMagick may be older than the MinimumVersion of its entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php.
  This minimum exists for unified command parameter ordering & alpha channel preservation & is not a security control.
  ImageMagick changed its core syntax rules at version 7.0, unifying legacy binaries under the single magick tool.
  The modern scheme strictly requires setting parameters after input declarations to prevent canvas flattening.
  HRConvert2 compares these values numerically down to the third patch depth, so all releases are ranked correctly.
  HRConvert2 refuses any build it cannot positively identify, because an unknown build cannot be cleared.
  Check the installed version by running '/usr/bin/magick --version' as the web server user.
  The conversion sequence is aborted to protect the processing pipeline from invalid command syntax errors.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-8002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so this image conversion cannot be isolated!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Image/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An image conversion was refused because the server could not build a sandbox for it.
  ImageMagick parses untrusted image files & has a long history of memory corruption bugs.
  The delegate system invokes other programs by design, which is what ImageTragick abused.
  The policy the Image pipeline carries into each conversion restricts that natively, but a
  policy file is a blocklist & has been bypassed.
  HRConvert2 therefore runs the conversion inside a bubblewrap namespace that can see only
  the two directories the conversion needs, & refuses rather than running without one.
  Bubblewrap requires unprivileged user namespaces, which several situations prevent.
  The bubblewrap package may not be installed.
  Ubuntu 23.10 & later set kernel.apparmor_restrict_unprivileged_userns to 1, which blocks
  bubblewrap unless it runs under an AppArmor profile that permits a user namespace.
  From v4.0.5 every sandbox runs HRConvert2's own copy of bubblewrap, at
  /usr/local/libexec/hrconvert2/bwrap, which the hrconvert2-bwrap profile -fp writes is attached
  to. --setup --install-depends & --update-depends install the copy, & -v says where it is
  missing or cannot be run. Where bubblewrap refuses a sandbox twice, -fp lifts the restriction,
  unless --Allow Unprivileged Namespaces-- is FALSE. It never lifts it for an attempt cut short,
  or to cover a copy that is missing or cannot be run.
  A container blocks the required syscalls unless it was started with the correct options.
  See --Require Sandbox-- & --Require Sandbox On Docker-- in config.php.
  From v4.2.7 a sandbox profile that sets RequireSandbox is refused whatever those settings say.
  The libreoffice, calibre, inkscape, resvg, dia, meshlab & ffmpeg profiles set it, because each
  tool reads a file an upload names by its path, & -v names them beside Sandbox required.
  From v4.3.1 the imagemagick, tesseract & mat2 profiles set it too, so an image conversion is
  refused here whatever those settings say. Until v4.3.1 it ran without a sandbox where they allowed.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  to confirm whether this server has a working sandbox, & how.
  Documentation/Build/diagnose-bwrap.sh probes it the way HRConvert2 does, & changes nothing.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-8003, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A file named pdf that holds no PDF was refused, so ImageMagick never read it!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Image/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  From v4.3.0 ImageMagick reads an image with the decoder the imageMagickDecoders.php shared module names, & with no other.
  A decoder is named by what a file holds, & by its extension where what it holds names none. The PDF decoder hands the file to
  Ghostscript, which reads PostScript as readily as a PDF, so a file named pdf is read as one only where its first bytes are a
  PDF's header, & the file was refused because they were not. A file named pdf that holds an image is read as that image.
  The file may be damaged, or PostScript, or something else named pdf. Nothing was converted, & nothing was run.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-9000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The model converter produced no file after <ADDITIONAL_DATA> attempt(s)!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Model/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Assimp or MeshLab, by the route the pipeline chose, ran & left no file. From v4.3.1 it is asked again only where something outside it
  stopped it, & from v4.3.2 that is KILL or TERM alone, as a memory ceiling or a supervisor stops a converter. One that crashed, with SEGV,
  ABRT, BUS, FPE or ILL, is not asked again, because it would crash again on the same file. Until v4.3.1 it was asked as many times as
  --File Operation Retry Count-- allows whatever happened, & until v4.3.2 a crash was asked again too.
  Until v4.0.6 this read The model converter timed out! No time limit ever caused it, & one more attempt was made after it.
  With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the entry immediately above this one carries what Assimp or MeshLab, by the route the pipeline chose, printed, which usually names the cause.
  A model neither can read is the usual cause. On a two stage route the second converter runs only where the first left a model for it.
  A converter its memory ceiling killed ends the same way. With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the Conversion Limit entry names that ceiling, & --Maximum Per Conversion Resources-- in config.php sets the most each type may be given.
  From v4.3.2 Assimp is also held to an address space of the Model type's memory, by the assimp sandbox profile, & a model that asks for
  more fails at once, & Assimp's output says std::bad_alloc. A 20 byte glb asked for 4.1 GiB before it failed.
  The warning below this names the command that was run, so it can be run again by hand. It names the conversion's own folder, which is
  taken away when the conversion ends, so the input must be put back in its in folder to run it.
  A model refused for what it names outside itself is not this error. A warning says why, & no number follows it, because an upload
  caused it & it was handled. A glTF whose buffer is named by a path, or whose header is 128 bytes or longer, whose image is named by a
  path, whose glb chunk ends past the file, whose JSON names a key twice in one object, or is larger than a quarter of the memory the
  request has left, is refused before Assimp opens it. So is an OBJ joining its lines in a way no program writes, or with a line longer
  than 8 MiB. An OBJ's material library named by a path is left out of the copy Assimp reads, & a warning counts the lines left out.
  From v4.3.3 an OFF is refused before MeshLab opens it unless it is a header of ST, C & N before OFF & three counts, a line for each
  vertex & a line for each face, naming 3 or 4 different vertices the file holds, & any colour is 3 or 4 numbers. The warning says
  MeshLab never opened it.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-9001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed Assimp version is missing, unidentifiable, or too old!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Model/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Assimp is one of the engines behind every 3D model format conversion, & a route that reads or writes obj, fbx, gltf, glb, 3ds, dae,
  x3d, 3mf, x, assbin or json cannot run without it. From v4.3.2 a route MeshLab alone runs never asks for it.
  From v4.3.2 the Assimp used is HRConvert2's own build alone, which Documentation/Build/build-assimp.sh makes under
  /usr/local/libexec/hrconvert2/builds/assimp, because its Fallback in Resources/Contract/depends.php says 'Always' => TRUE. The package
  manager's Assimp is never run, whatever version it is, so this is raised where the own build has not been made, & installing
  assimp-utils does not clear it. Run  sudo php convertCore.php --setup --install-depends --subsystem="3D Models"  to build it. It
  installs a compiler, CMake & git where there are none, needs a route to GitHub, & takes about a minute & a half on two x86 cores.
  The own build reports 6.0.6, from its patch 0008, & its entry's MinimumVersion is 6.0.6, so a build that reports less is built again.
  HRConvert2 compares these values numerically, so all releases are ranked correctly, & refuses any build it cannot positively
  identify, because an unknown build cannot be cleared.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  to see what this installation has, & --setup --check-depends to see whether the own build is in service.

  The minimum is the MinimumVersion of its entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-9003, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so this model conversion cannot be isolated!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Model/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A 3D model conversion was refused because the server could not build a sandbox for it.
  Assimp & MeshLab both parse complex binary scene formats & both carry CVE histories.
  Neither offers a native restriction on what it may read, so the sandbox is the only
  boundary available & the conversion is refused rather than run without one.
  Assimp reads a glTF's buffer from wherever the glTF names it & writes it into the model it makes. The assimp sandbox profile,
  from v4.3.2, & the meshlab profile, from v4.2.7, both set RequireSandbox, so --Require Sandbox-- & --Require Sandbox On Docker--
  do not decide this. Until v4.3.2 Assimp ran under the meshlab profile.
  See error 8002 for the full list of reasons a sandbox may be unavailable & how to fix it.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-9004, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Neither Assimp nor MeshLab can write the <FILE_TYPE> format!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Model/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A model conversion was requested into a format that no installed utility can produce.
  From v4.3.2 Assimp writes obj, 3ds, dae, x3d, fbx, gltf, glb, 3mf, x, assbin & json, & MeshLab writes stl, ply, off, dxf, wrl & ctm.
  Until then Assimp wrote stl & ply too, through its PreTransformVertices, which a crafted model makes crash.
  A format in neither list cannot be produced, however the conversion is routed. u3d is one.
  This is reported before anything runs, because retrying a command that cannot succeed only delays the same failure.
  READING A FORMAT IS NOT THE SAME AS WRITING IT.
  Assimp reads more formats than it writes, & MeshLab writes formats Assimp cannot. Error 9005 is the same refusal for a format
  neither reads.
  An earlier version routed on the input extension alone, so an obj to off conversion reached Assimp, which answered that it could not guess the output format, and the retry loop repeated the identical command until it timed out with error 9000.
  Remove the format from --Supported Model Output Formats-- in config.php, or install a utility that can write it.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-9005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Neither Assimp nor MeshLab reads the <FILE_TYPE> format here!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Model/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A model was uploaded in a format neither of the two reads, from v4.3.2, & nothing was run.
  Each upload is read by one program. HRConvert2's own Assimp reads obj, fbx, gltf & glb, with the three importers it is built with,
  & MeshLab reads stl, ply, off, 3ds, dae, x3d & ctm. Until v4.3.2 Assimp read every model, with every importer a distribution
  builds, & it chose an importer by what a file holds where none claimed its extension, so any of seventy was reachable from an upload.
  x, 3mf & dxf are read by neither from v4.3.2. Assimp writes x & 3mf, & MeshLab writes dxf. The three leave the stock
  --Supported Model Input Formats-- & --Supported Model Formats--. config.php stays v4.2.8, so an existing one keeps them, & a model
  in one is refused here or, where the pipeline no longer declares the format, by error 34005. Remove the three from the two
  settings by hand. A dxf is still read by the Drawing pipeline, as a drawing.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-9007, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not move the converted model to <FILE_NAME>! <REASON>

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Model/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The converter wrote the model in the conversion's own folder, & it could not be moved into the user's folder, from v4.3.2.
  Only the output leaves that folder, & a gltf's buffer with it. Each must be a plain file, because a link moved into the user's
  folder would be followed when it is downloaded, & a buffer takes its name by a link, which fails rather than replaces a name taken
  since the conversion began. The reason says which of these it was. Nothing of the user's was written over, & the conversion's
  folder was taken away with what the converter wrote in it.
  Until v4.3.2 a converter wrote straight into the user's folder, & a gltf's buffer replaced a file of the same name there.
  The user's folder may be full, or the account running the conversion may not write to it. -fp repairs the permissions.

FILE

FATAL
  NO

  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION

  another name. The pipeline asks the Assimp the manifest names for the extensions it imports, with  assimp listext , before it
  converts anything, & an Assimp with any other importer is refused here, & nothing was run.
  Assimp chooses an importer by what a file holds where none claims its extension, so every importer it carries is reachable from an
  upload, & a distribution's Assimp carries seventy. HRConvert2's own build carries three, which were reviewed for v4.3.2.
  Dependency Core never uses a package manager's Assimp, because its Fallback says 'Always' => TRUE. This is raised where an operator
  named another program in the Binary of the Assimp entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php, or the own build was replaced by hand.
  Put the Binary back to assimp & run  sudo php convertCore.php --setup --install-depends --subsystem="3D Models" .

  The minimum is the MinimumVersion of its entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php.

  Dia is one of the few dependencies whose major version is still zero. It has been at
  0.98 since 2014, so this minimum exists to make a MISSING binary produce a named error
  rather than a timeout, not because any known version is unsuitable.
  A build that reports no parseable version is refused, because an unknown build cannot be
  cleared against a minimum.
  This check did not exist before v3.7. Dia was invoked blind, & a missing binary produced
  error 10000, a converter timeout, after the full retry count had elapsed.
  NOTE. Dia is a GTK application. If a drawing conversion fails INSIDE a working sandbox
  rather than producing this error, the cause is usually a display requirement. The
  sandbox provides a private /tmp, so an X socket outside it is not visible.

  Run  php convertCore.php -v  to confirm whether this server has a working sandbox.

ERROR MESSAGE

  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An archive no archiver was run for. One that ran & left nothing raises its format's own number, 13001 through 13005 or 13100, & from v4.0.6 not this one as well.
  An archive the extraction refused raises it, after the warning that says why, such as a password, no files, or more than --Maximum Extracted Size-- or --Maximum Extracted Files-- allows.
  One or more of the following dependencies are not responding: Rar, Unar, Tar, Zip, Unzip, or 7zipper.
  The conversion operation failed to produce an output file.
  The user submitted a request to convert a file that does not exist.
  The user submitted a request that was incorrect, malformed, or corrupt.
  The user has requested to convert a file that was deleted for exceeding the --File Deletion Age Threshold-- specified in config.php.
  The path to the Installation directory specified in the --Installation Directory-- section of the config.php file does not exist or cannot be accessed.
  The path to the Data directory specified in the --Data Storage Directory-- section of the config.php file does not exist or cannot be accessed.
  The web server account needs read access to the installation, & read & write access to DATA in the hosted location.
  The web server account needs read & write access to Sessions, Cache, Logs & Sockets in the data location, which -fp gives it. The data location itself is root's.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the directory where the application is installed.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the directory where Data is stored.
  This may be the result of incorrect user or group associations for the www-data user or group.
  The device where data is stored has an insufficient amount of storage space available.
  The request contained character types or encoded data that are not supported.
  One of the $_POST or $_GET variables submitted was empty after sanitization.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The archiver produced no archive after <ADDITIONAL_DATA> attempt(s)!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  7-Zip ran & left no archive. From v4.3.1 it is asked again only where a signal stopped it,
  as many times as --File Operation Retry Count-- allows. Until then it was asked that many times whatever happened.
  Until v4.0.6 this read The archiver timed out! No time limit ever caused it, & one more attempt was made after it.
  With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the entry immediately above this one carries what 7-Zip printed, which usually names the cause.
  Error 13000 follows it, because the operation produced nothing.
  A converter its memory ceiling killed ends the same way. With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the Conversion Limit entry names that ceiling, & --Maximum Per Conversion Resources-- in config.php sets the most each type may be given.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so this archive operation cannot be isolated!

FILE

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An archive operation was refused because the server could not build a sandbox for it.
  This error covers three separate operations.
  Extracting a source archive during a format conversion.
  Re-archiving the extracted files into the requested output format.
  Archiving a single file the user selected, which is a different operation entirely.
  An archive utility parses attacker supplied structure & has historically been a source of

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13007, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The extractor produced no files from the source archive!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The source archive was opened but nothing came out of it, so there is nothing to convert.
  The archive may be empty, corrupt, encrypted, or in a format 7-Zip cannot decompress even
  though it can read the container.
  THE MOST COMMON CAUSE IS A MISSING CODEC RATHER THAN A BROKEN ARCHIVE.
  On Debian & Ubuntu, rar extraction requires the p7zip-rar package IN ADDITION to p7zip-full.
  Without it, 7-Zip reads the archive headers perfectly, reports Unsupported Method for every
  member, & extracts nothing. Install it with  sudo apt install p7zip-rar
  Confirm the codec is present by running  7z i  & looking for rar in the list.
  HRConvert2's own 7-Zip, built where the package manager's is too old, carries the rar codec
  itself & needs no package for it.
  The version check cannot detect a missing codec, because the version reported is correct.
  This check exists because an empty extraction used to be re-archived silently. The
  archiver packaged the empty staging folder, the output file existed, & the conversion
  reported success while producing an archive containing nothing.
  The extractor output is written to the log immediately above this error & names the
  specific reason for each member it could not extract.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13008, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed 7-Zip version is missing, unidentifiable, or too old!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php
  /Resources/Pipelines/Archiver/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  7-Zip could not be verified. It is used in three places & this error covers all of them.
  It is the ONLY extractor, so every archive INPUT format depends on it regardless of what
  the output format is. A conversion cannot begin without it.
  It creates 7z, cbz & cbr archives during a format conversion.
  It creates 7z, tar, tar.gz & tar.bz2 archives when a single file is archived.
  NOTE. Those two lists differ. A format conversion uses the tar utility for the tar family
  & 7-Zip for 7z, while a single file archive uses 7-Zip for both. This is a historical
  inconsistency rather than a deliberate design & is worth being aware of when diagnosing a
  format that works in one path & not the other.
  Install it with  sudo php convertCore.php --setup --install-depends --subsystem=Archives
  The minimum is 26.02 & is set in Resources/Contract/depends.php. Every 7-Zip before it decodes
  XZ with a heap overflow a crafted archive reaches. Where the package manager carries an older
  one, as Ubuntu 24.04 carries 23.01, that command builds HRConvert2's own from the source 7-Zip
  publishes into /usr/local/libexec/hrconvert2/builds/7-zip, & every part of HRConvert2 runs it.
  See ABOUT_DEPENDENCIES.txt.
  Check which 7-Zip is used, & its version, with
  sudo php convertCore.php --setup --check-depends --subsystem=Archives
  A build that reports no parseable version is refused, because an unknown build cannot be
  cleared against a minimum. An own build with any part an account other than root could change
  is never run, & one the web server's account cannot run, because a folder above it is closed to
  that account or its filesystem runs no programs, is not used. --check-depends run as root calls
  either misconfigured & says why.
  NOTE. This check verifies the VERSION & cannot verify the CODECS. A correctly versioned
  7-Zip with no rar codec passes this check & then silently fails to extract rar archives.
  See error 13007 for that case.
  NOTE. 7-Zip cannot create rar archives. See error 13012.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13009, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Mkisofs is missing, unidentifiable, or too old!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php
  /Resources/Pipelines/Archiver/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Mkisofs could not be verified, so disk image formats cannot be created.
  This affects OUTPUT only, both when converting an archive & when archiving a single file.
  A format conversion can produce iso, vhd & vdi. A single file archive can produce iso.
  Those formats can still be READ as input, because 7-Zip performs every extraction.
  The command may be provided by cdrtools or by the genisoimage fork. Either is accepted.
  Install it with  sudo apt install genisoimage
  The minimum is the MinimumVersion of its entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php & is deliberately low,
  because the two implementations use unrelated version numbers.
  Check the installed version by running  mkisofs --version  in a terminal.
  Remove iso, vhd & vdi from --Supported Archive Formats-- in config.php if this server does not
  need to create disk images, & the formats will not be offered to users at all.

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archiver/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Zip could not be verified, so a file cannot be archived as zip.
  This affects archiving a selection of files only. An archive converted to zip is written by 7-Zip,
  & zip archives can still be READ as input, because 7-Zip performs every extraction.
  Until v4.1.7 the Archive pipeline raised it too, because Zip wrote an archive converted to zip.
  Install it with  sudo apt install zip
  The minimum is the MinimumVersion of its entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php.
  Check the installed version by running  zip -v  in a terminal.
  The zip format has been stable for a very long time, so this minimum exists for
  consistency with the other dependency checks rather than because any known version is
  unsuitable. A failure here almost always means the utility is not installed at all.
