----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13013, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Rar output requires the rar utility, which is missing or too old!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A rar archive was requested & this server cannot create one.
  THERE IS NO FALLBACK FOR THIS FORMAT & THERE CANNOT BE ONE.
  RAR compression is proprietary. 7-Zip can READ a rar archive but has never been able to
  WRITE one, so every other archive format in HRConvert2 has an alternative & this one does
  not. An earlier version attempted to fall back to 7-Zip here, which produced an error
  rather than an archive.
  Rar INPUT is unaffected. 7-Zip extracts rar archives normally, provided the rar codec is
  present. See error 13007 for that case.
  The rar utility is a commercial product from RARLAB & is not packaged by Debian or Ubuntu.
  The binary that is commonly installed is a trial copy which prints a registration notice
  into the log on every archive it creates. That notice is harmless & is not an error.
  Install it from https://www.rarlab.com/download.htm
  The minimum is the MinimumVersion of its entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php.
  Check the installed version by running  rar  with no arguments in a terminal.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  to see whether this server has a usable rar utility.
  Remove rar from --Supported Archive Formats-- in config.php if this server cannot create rar
  archives, & the format will no longer be offered to users at all. That is the correct
  resolution on a server that will never have the utility installed.
  NOTE. HRConvert2 passes -ma4 when creating a rar archive, which selects the older RAR4
  format. RAR 7.00 defaults to a compression method that older extractors report as
  unsupported, & without -ma4 this application produced archives it could not itself
  extract on the very next request.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13014, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The extracted archive could not be checked for unsafe links, so it was not re-archived!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Every archive is extracted into a staging folder & that folder is walked before it is
  re-archived, so that a link which does not resolve to a file inside it can be removed.
  This error means the walk could not finish, so the folder holds links this conversion
  cannot account for & it is refused rather than packaged.
  The archive is not produced. Nothing is sent to the user.
  The walk fails when the staging folder cannot be resolved, or when a link that has to be
  removed cannot be unlinked. Both point at file permissions on the data location rather
  than at the archive that was uploaded.
  The web server account needs read & write access to Sessions in the data location, which -fp gives it.
  A link that is simply removed is not this error. That is a warning carrying a count, & the
  conversion continues normally.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13105, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Cryptographic validation failed for internal boot assets! Files are missing or corrupted.

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A static asset required to make a bootable image, such as the image of a UEFI system partition, failed its integrity check.
  The targeted virtual filesystem image file may be missing from the internal $BootloadersDir storage path.
  The image may have been altered, damaged during deployment, or truncated during a standard update cycle.
  The computed file hash did not match the hash pinned for it in verifyIsoBootloaders(), in Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php.
  HRConvert2 refuses any asset it cannot positively validate to guarantee deployment container security.
  Verify file integrity by calculating 'sha256sum' on the files inside the bootloaders directory.
  This restriction exists to protect media consistency and ensure predictable sector routing formats.
  Image operations manipulate local filesystem structures and never reach out to remote hosts.
  A compromised or mismatching base image cannot bypass verification to reach the sandbox pipeline.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13106, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not generate required bootloader assets!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The pipeline failed to process, format, or inject the necessary files into the workspace prior to compiling.
  The host environment may be running low on transient space or has encountered storage block access restrictions.
  An internal file system failure may have occurred while attempting to duplicate a pre-baked target image asset.
  The global $BootloadersDir configuration path might point to a structure lacking readable directory boundaries.
  HRConvert2 throws this alert to stop mkisofs from compiling incomplete structural configurations.
  Verify host platform conditions and ensure appropriate storage tracking limits are assigned to scratch paths.
  This restriction acts as a processing firewall to safeguard filesystem parameters from empty calculations.
  Workspace operations write changes directly onto local data pathways and do not communicate externally.
  A disrupted copy operation safely drops execution logic blocks before reaching the sandboxed compiler phase.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13107, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A hybrid bootable image requires the isohybrid utility from syslinux-utils, which is missing or too old!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A hybrid image was requested & isohybrid could not be used to produce one.
  A hybrid image boots from optical media & from a USB device, which is what the hybrid in the
  name refers to. A plain image was not written in its place, because an image that does not
  boot the way it was asked to is worse than no image at all.
  Install syslinux-utils, then run -v & read the Syslinux Utils line for the version found.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-13108, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: No free name was found for the bootable image <FILE_NAME>, so it was not written!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A bootable image is asked for with an extension naming its architecture, & is written as an iso.
  An iso of that name was already in the session, & so was every name from _1 to _1000 after it.
  A taken name is given the next free one so that nothing a user already has is replaced, & a
  thousand copies of one name means something other than a person is converting.
  Delete the copies that are no longer wanted, or choose another name.

  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the directory where the application is installed.
  This may be the result of incorrect user or group associations for the www-data user or group.
  The device where data is stored has an insufficient amount of storage space available.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-14005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The output for <FILE_NAME> would replace the original, so it was refused!

FILE
  /convertCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An operation was about to write to the very file it was reading.
  An output whose name is taken is given the next free name, & the original holds its own name, so
  a conversion never reaches this. It remains for an output an operation adds to after naming it,
  such as an archive gaining its second file, which must never be one of the files being read.
  Until v4.0.0 a stale output was removed before an operation wrote a new one, & when the output
  & the original were the same path that removed the only copy.
  The file is refused & the operation reports it as a file it could not verify.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-14006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: No free name was found for the output of <FILE_NAME>, so it was refused!

FILE
  /convertCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The name asked for an output was already in the session, & so was every name from _1 to _1000
  after it.
  A taken name is given the next free one so that nothing a user already has is replaced, & a
  thousand copies of one name means something other than a person is converting.
  The file is refused & the operation reports it as a file it could not verify. Delete the copies
  that are no longer wanted, or choose another name.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-15011, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not create a file at <PATH>!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/OCR/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The conversion ran & no output file exists at the path it was told to write.
  This is the converter reporting success while producing nothing, which is a different fault
  from a converter that failed & said so.
  The usual cause is a permission on the data location. Run -fp as root, which corrects
  ownership on every path this application manages.
  A full disk produces the same result. Check the free space on the data location.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-15012, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so this OCR operation cannot be isolated!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/OCR/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An OCR operation was refused because the server could not build a sandbox for it.
  This error covers pdftotext & Tesseract. Neither offers a native restriction on what it
  may read, so the sandbox is the only boundary available.
  A PDF is a container format that can reference external resources & embed fonts, scripts
  and images, so a PDF parser is a considerably larger attack surface than it appears.
  See error 8002 for the full list of reasons a sandbox may be unavailable & how to fix it.
  The ImageMagick step of the advanced OCR method, which splits a PDF into pages, raises this error
  too, from v4.3.1, & nothing after it is attempted for that file. Until v4.3.1 it ran without a
  sandbox under the policy the OCR pipeline carries, with a warning, even where config.php required
  one. The tesseract profile sets RequireSandbox from v4.3.1, so --Require Sandbox-- & --Require
  Sandbox On Docker-- do not decide this.
  NOTE. A document is turned into a PDF first by LibreOffice, in a sandbox of its own, & a
  refusal there is error 2004.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  to confirm whether this server has a working sandbox.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-15013, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed Tesseract version is missing, unidentifiable, or too old!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/OCR/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Tesseract could not be verified, so text cannot be read out of an image.
  This affects two routes. Reading an image directly, & reading the rasterized pages of a
  PDF during the advanced OCR method.
  Reading a PDF that already holds a text layer is NOT affected. That route uses pdftotext.
  Install it with  sudo apt install tesseract-ocr
  The minimum is the MinimumVersion of its entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php & is a REAL floor
  rather than a formality.
  Version 3 used a character classifier. Version 4 replaced it with an LSTM engine, & the
  difference in output quality is large enough that a version 3 result reads as a broken
  conversion rather than a poor one. A user who does not know which engine ran would
  reasonably report it as a bug.
  Version 5 is the current series & is what a modern distribution ships.
  Check the installed version by running  tesseract --version  in a terminal.
  The banner lists leptonica, libpng, zlib & a dozen other library versions. The version
  being checked is the one on the FIRST line, immediately after the product name.
  A build that reports no parseable version is refused, because an unknown build cannot be
  cleared against a minimum.
  This check did not exist before v3.7. Tesseract was invoked blind, & a missing binary
  produced an empty result & a conversion that reported failure without naming a cause.
  NOTE. A missing language data package produces a DIFFERENT failure that this check
  cannot detect. Tesseract itself is present & current, & the conversion still produces
  nothing. Install tesseract-ocr-<lang> for every language the server needs to read.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-15014, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed pdftotext version is missing, unidentifiable, or too old!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/OCR/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  pdftotext could not be verified, so an existing text layer cannot be extracted from a PDF.
  pdftotext is part of the poppler-utils package rather than being a package of its own.
  Install it with  sudo apt install poppler-utils
  The minimum is the MinimumVersion of its entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php.
  poppler uses a date style version where the major is the YEAR & the minor is the MONTH,
  so 24.02 is February 2024 rather than version twenty four point two.
  Check the installed version by running  pdftotext -v  in a terminal.
  This minimum exists to make a MISSING binary produce a named error rather than a
  timeout, not because any known version is unsuitable.
  A build that reports no parseable version is refused, because an unknown build cannot be
  cleared against a minimum.
  THIS ERROR IS NOT ALWAYS FATAL TO THE OPERATION.
  When it occurs during OCR method 1, the operation falls through to method 2, which
  rasterizes the page & reads it with Tesseract instead. A server with no poppler can
  still OCR a PDF, just more slowly & with less exact output.
  When it occurs during the image fallback route, there is no alternative & the operation
  fails.
  This check did not exist before v3.7. pdftotext was invoked blind.
  NOTE. pdftotext cannot read a scanned page. A PDF with no text layer produces nothing at
  all even when pdftotext is present & current, which is the entire reason method 2 exists.
  An empty result is not necessarily a fault in this dependency.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-15015, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A file named pdf that holds no PDF was refused, so ImageMagick never read it!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/OCR/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  OCR method 2 rasterizes a PDF's pages with ImageMagick. From v4.3.0 ImageMagick reads them with the decoder the
  imageMagickDecoders.php shared module names, & a file named pdf is read as a PDF only where its first bytes are a PDF's header,
  because Ghostscript reads PostScript as readily as a PDF. Error 8003 says the same of the Image pipeline.
  The file may be damaged, or PostScript, or something else named pdf. No page was rasterized, & the file was not read.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-17002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: ClamAV is missing, too old, or unusable, so the user virus scan could not run!
  RETIRED AT v3.9.9. This number is no longer raised. A user requested scan goes through runVirusScan
  & the ClamAV pipeline, which reports an unusable ClamAV as error 502. The report the user downloads
  still says the file could not be scanned & that the scan DID NOT RUN. The number is reserved & not reused.

FILE
  /convertCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A user requested an on-demand ClamAV scan & clamscan could not be located on the server.
  A user requested an on-demand ClamAV scan & clamscan reported a version older than
  the minimum Resources/Contract/depends.php gives it.
  A user requested an on-demand ClamAV scan & clamscan did not report a version that could be read.
  NO FILE WAS SCANNED. No clean result is implied by this error & none should be inferred.
  The scan log the user downloads carries a matching line saying the scan did not run, so the user
  is told plainly rather than being shown an empty report that reads as a clean one.
  The scan is reported to the user as a failed operation.
  Install ClamAV, or disable --User Virus Scanning-- in config.php if on-demand scanning is not wanted.

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The path to the Installation directory specified in the --Installation Directory-- section of the config.php file does not exist or cannot be accessed.
  The web server account needs read access to the installation, & read & write access to DATA in the hosted location.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the directory where the application is installed.
  This may be the result of incorrect user or group associations for the www-data user or group.
  The device where data is stored has an insufficient amount of storage space available.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WARNINGS ABOUT SCANNING FILES FOR A USER
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  The Scan pipeline raises these, from /Resources/Pipelines/Scan/pipeline.php.
    WARNING!!! <SCANNER> could not scan <FILE_NAME> for a user.
      The scanner reached no verdict on that file. The report says the file could not be scanned, & never that it is clean.
      The scanner's own warning, or -v, says why. A scan that reached no verdict on any file ends with error 23.
    WARNING!!! Potentially infected file detected at <FILE_NAME>!
      The scanner found the file infected. The report says so, & the file is left where it is for the user to see.
      A workflow's Scan step that finds one ends the workflow, so nothing is made from the file.
      From v4.2.3 ScanCore v1.9 finds a decompression bomb infected, & PHP hidden in an image, & a known hash inside an archive.
    WARNING!!! Suspicious file detected at <FILE_NAME>.
      From v4.2.3. ScanCore matched the file to a definition that is not a hash & is not marked high confidence, such as a host,
      a URL, a run of bytes or a name. That is a heuristic, & a suspicious file is not an infected one. The report says so, under
      the line ScanCore wrote, & the file is left where it is. A workflow's Scan step that finds one goes on, & names it in its result.
      The definitions ScanCore ships are hashes alone, so a match by content is raised only by a definition an administrator added.
      From v4.2.3 ScanCore v1.9 raises it for a structure too, such as a malformed archive, an archive hidden behind an image or a
      file named as a png that is not one.
    WARNING!!! <SCANNER> could not inspect all of <FILE_NAME>.
      From v4.2.3. ScanCore v1.9 could not read the whole file, & wrote Incomplete with the reason, such as an encrypted member of a
      zip, a bzip2 stream, a damaged archive or one larger than its budget, or logged its error 610 for a file it could not open once
      a scan had begun, or its error 600 for one it could not read to the end. A file not scanned in full is not known to be clean.
      The report says so, under the line ScanCore wrote, & the file is left where it is. A workflow's Scan step that finds one goes on,
      & names it in its result. A file also found infected or suspicious is named only as that. A file ScanCore cannot read at all it
      refuses before it scans, & that is the first warning above, that it could not scan the file. Nothing needs to change unless
      every file is named so, which can mean the PHP zlib extension is missing, without which ScanCore reads no compressed member.
    WARNING!!! A scan was asked to write a report under a name that is not a plain text file's name, so it was written as <FILE_NAME>.
      From v4.1.6. The report is always a txt file, & a name for it that is not one is replaced rather than trusted.
      The core names every report User_Consolidated_Virus_Scan_Report.txt, so this means something other than the core
      dispatched the Scan pipeline with a name of its own.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-21000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The stream converter failed to launch!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Stream/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Stream conversions are now launched in the background so the user can be served immediately.
  A backgrounded process cannot time out during the request, so that condition no longer exists here.
  A stream that runs too long is now terminated by the stream supervisor after --Stream Duration Timeout--.
  This error means the FFMPEG process was never started at all and no process identifier was returned.
  FFMPEG may not be installed, or may not be reachable on the system path used by the web server user.
  The web server user may not have permission to spawn background processes on this system.
  The shell used by PHP may not support the background operator or the process identifier echo.
  This error occurs only after the stream file has already passed full inspection.
  It also follows the WARNING!!! that the stream converter was not launched, because no sandbox
  could be built. From v4.2.7 the ffmpeg sandbox profile sets RequireSandbox, so that happens
  wherever no sandbox can be built, whatever --Require Sandbox-- & --Require Sandbox On Docker-- say.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-21001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Stream inspection denied this file. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>.

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Stream/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A stream file was inspected before conversion and something in it was refused.
  <ADDITIONAL_DATA> will be replaced with the specific reason the inspection stopped.
  Stream files may reference remote hosts, and HRConvert2 validates every reference before FFMPEG sees the file.
  The file may reference a private, reserved, loopback, or link-local address.
  The file may reference a hostname that resolves to a private address, which is a DNS rebinding pattern.
  The file may reference a protocol other than http or https, such as file, gopher, concat, or subfile.
  The file may reference plain http while --Allow Streams Over HTTP-- is disabled in config.php.
  The file may contain more references than --Stream Inspection Files Per Layer-- permits.
  The file may nest playlists deeper than --Stream Inspection Layers-- permits.
  With --Stream Inspection Layers-- at 0 nothing a file references is fetched, so one that references anything is refused,
  unless --Default Stream Inspection Forfeit Action-- is ALLOW. The uploaded file itself is always inspected.
  An address in a range --Permitted Private Ranges-- names is not refused, whether the file names it as a host or as an address.
  The file may be a playlist disguised with a .ts extension, or content of an unrecognized type.
  Until v4.3.0 an uploaded ts was refused here whatever it held, because its bytes were read for addresses as a playlist's are, & an
  encoder's note such as x264's web address is one. A segment is now classified from its first packets & read for nothing more.
  A relative reference in an uploaded playlist cannot be resolved, because an upload has no source URL to inherit from.
  Playlists downloaded from a content delivery network commonly use relative references and must be edited to use absolute URLs.
  Consult the Verbose log entries immediately preceding this error for the layer and file involved.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-21002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed FFMPEG version is missing, unidentifiable, or vulnerable to stream playlist protocol bypass!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Stream/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The FFMPEG dependency required to convert stream files could not be cleared for stream work.
  FFMPEG builds from v2.0 through v6.0 apply their own protocol whitelist to segments referenced inside a playlist.
  The whitelist HRConvert2 supplies to FFMPEG is therefore bypassed on those builds.
  Stream inspection cannot protect an affected build, so stream conversion is refused outright.
  It is a security control, & one minimum now covers every FFMPEG conversion, so it is this one.
  The threshold is the MinimumVersion of the FFMPEG entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php & must never be lowered below 6.1.
  The installed FFMPEG may be a development or git build which does not report a parseable version number.
  HRConvert2 refuses any build it cannot positively identify, because an unknown build cannot be cleared.
  Verify the installed version by running 'ffmpeg -version' as the web server user.
  Audio and video conversions will continue to work on an affected build, because neither fetches remote content.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-22000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The subtitle converter produced no file after <ADDITIONAL_DATA> attempt(s)!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Subtitle/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  FFMPEG ran & left no file. From v4.3.1 it is asked again only where a signal stopped it,
  as many times as --File Operation Retry Count-- allows. Until then it was asked that many times whatever happened.
  Until v4.0.6 this read The subtitle converter timed out! No time limit ever caused it, & one more attempt was made after it.
  With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the entry immediately above this one carries what FFMPEG printed, which usually names the cause.
  A subtitle file FFMPEG cannot read is the usual cause.
  A converter its memory ceiling killed ends the same way. With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the Conversion Limit entry names that ceiling, & --Maximum Per Conversion Resources-- in config.php sets the most each type may be given.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-22001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so this subtitle conversion cannot be isolated!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Subtitle/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A subtitle conversion was refused because the server could not build a sandbox for it.
  FFMPEG performs subtitle conversions & parses untrusted text & binary subtitle formats.
  A subtitle conversion needs no network access, so the sandbox unshares it entirely.
  FFMPEG reads a DASH manifest whatever the upload is called, & opens each file it names.
  From v4.2.7 the ffmpeg sandbox profile sets RequireSandbox, so --Require Sandbox-- &
  --Require Sandbox On Docker-- do not decide this.
  See error 8002 for the full list of reasons a sandbox may be unavailable & how to fix it.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  to confirm whether this server has a working sandbox.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-22002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed FFMPEG version is missing, unidentifiable, or too old!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Subtitle/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  FFMPEG could not be verified, so subtitle conversions cannot be performed.
  FFMPEG performs every subtitle conversion. It is the same binary used for audio & video.
  The GENERAL minimum is enforced here rather than the stream minimum, because a subtitle
  conversion reads a local file & never fetches anything remote.
  The minimum is the MinimumVersion of its entry in Resources/Contract/depends.php.
  Check the installed version by running  ffmpeg -version  in a terminal.
  A git build reports a commit hash where the version belongs & is refused, because an
  unknown build cannot be cleared against a minimum.
  This check did not exist before v3.7. Subtitle conversion was the only FFMPEG route with
  no version gate at all, & a missing binary produced error 22000, a converter timeout,
  after the full retry count had elapsed.
  See error 12001 for the audio route & 11001 for the video route. They enforce the same
  minimum & differ only in which conversion reported the failure.
  See error 21002 for the stream route, which enforces a HIGHER minimum because a stream
  fetches remote content & needs a build carrying the protocol handling fixes.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WARNINGS ABOUT FILES
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  The Files pipeline does the work on disk under listing, placing, renaming & deleting a session's files, & is handed the one
  directory it may touch. It refuses anything that resolves outside that directory, so a refusal here is the guard working.
  The Files pipeline raises these, from Resources/Pipelines/Files/pipeline.php.
    WARNING!!! A file listing was requested for a directory that does not resolve.
    WARNING!!! A file description was requested for a directory that does not resolve.
    WARNING!!! A file placement was requested in a directory that does not resolve.
    WARNING!!! A file rename was requested in a directory that does not resolve. Nothing was renamed.
    WARNING!!! A file removal was requested in a directory that does not resolve. Nothing was removed.
      The session folder, or the temporary folder beside it, does not exist or cannot be reached. A session swept away while a
      page was still open does this, & the next request starts a new one. Anything else is the permissions of the data location.
    WARNING!!! A directory resolved & could not be read. The permissions may not allow it.
      The web server account needs read access to the session folder. Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp .
    WARNING!!! A file placement named a source that is not a readable file.
      What an operation made was gone, or unreadable, before it could be placed in the session. Nothing was placed.
    WARNING!!! A file placement named something that could not be sanitized. It was refused.
    WARNING!!! A file rename named something that could not be sanitized. It was refused.
    WARNING!!! A file removal named something that could not be sanitized. It was refused.
      A name that changes under sanitize() was built rather than picked from the list, so nothing was done with it.
    WARNING!!! A file placement resolved outside the directory it was permitted. It was refused.
    WARNING!!! A file removal resolved outside the directory it was permitted. It was refused.
      The name was a link, or the directory itself is one, leading somewhere else. Nothing outside the directory is touched.
      From v4.1.8 a name with nothing under it in the directory is passed over quietly, because a delete asks in the session &
      in the temporary folder & a file is usually in only one of them. Before v4.1.8 every delete raised this warning for the
      folder its file was never in.
    WARNING!!! A file rename named something that is not a file in the directory it was permitted. It was refused.
    WARNING!!! A file removal named something that is not a file. It was refused.
      The name was a folder, a link or something other than a plain file, so it was left as it is.
    WARNING!!! A file could not be placed. The permissions or the disk may not allow it.
    WARNING!!! A file could not be renamed. The permissions or the mount may not allow it.
    WARNING!!! A file could not be removed. The permissions or the mount may not allow it.
      The web server account needs read & write access to the session folder, & the disk must have room & be writable.
    WARNING!!! The Files pipeline was asked for an operation it does not have: <OPERATION>.
      The core asked for something this version of the pipeline does not do. The pipeline & the core are from different
      releases. Unpack the release again, & run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp .
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-25002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so this SVG conversion cannot be isolated!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/SVG/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An SVG conversion was refused because the server could not build a sandbox for it.
  Inkscape parses SVG, which is XML & can reference external entities, remote images &
  embedded scripts. An SVG parser is a considerably larger attack surface than it appears.
  Inkscape offers no native restriction on what it may read, so the sandbox is the only
  boundary available & the conversion is refused rather than run without one.
  Inkscape & Resvg each draw an image an SVG names by a relative or an absolute path, & Inkscape
  a shape from another SVG it names. From v4.2.7 the inkscape & resvg sandbox profiles set
  RequireSandbox, so --Require Sandbox-- & --Require Sandbox On Docker-- do not decide this.
  Resvg is asked first where it is installed, & is refused with a WARNING!!! from the sandbox.
  See error 8002 for the full list of reasons a sandbox may be unavailable & how to fix it.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  to confirm whether this server has a working sandbox.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-26000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not remove the build environment!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The Dockerfile or the Build directory could not be removed from the installation.
  This work is controlled by --Delete Build Environment-- in config.php & is done by root, from the -fp argument.
  Root only removes a file from a folder it trusts, & a WARNING entry just above this error names what was left & why.
  A Build directory root does not trust is left exactly as it is & is never entered.
  A link at Build or inside it is removed as a link. What it points at is never touched.
  The Build directory may contain subdirectories, which this routine does not remove.
  The build environment is removed directly and not through cleanFiles(), because it sits outside every approved cleanup root.
  Widening the cleanFiles() whitelist to include the application directory would make the recursive deleter far more dangerous.
  From v4.0.3 until v4.0.6 the routine looked for both in Resources/Engine/Cores rather than in the installation, so it removed nothing,
  printed NOT PRESENT & never raised this error. An installation that asked for the build environment to go keeps it until -fp of v4.0.6 runs.

  The CHANGELOG.txt or README.md file could not be removed from the installation.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-27000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not prepare the OpenSCAD sources for rendering!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Scad/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  HRConvert2 could not produce a sanitized copy of the OpenSCAD source it was asked to render.
  Every uploaded OpenSCAD source is sanitized into the ScadTemp directory before OpenSCAD is allowed to run.
  The whole upload set is sanitized rather than just the requested file, because a resolved include would otherwise reach an unfiltered source.
  One or more sources may have failed to read or failed to write, and those failures are reported separately.
  The ScadTemp directory inside the user session directory may be missing or may not be writable.
  Consult error 27001 and error 27006 in the log for the specific file that failed.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-27001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not stage the sanitized OpenSCAD source <FILE_NAME>!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Scad/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A sanitized copy of an uploaded OpenSCAD source could not be written to the ScadTemp directory.
  The number of bytes written did not match the number of bytes expected, or the write failed outright.
  The disk or the partition holding the data storage directory may be full.
  The www-data:www-data user:group may not have write access to the user session directory.
  The ScadTemp directory may not exist, and it is created by the required directory routine at the start of every request.
  HRConvert2 never modifies or replaces the source the user uploaded, so their original file is unaffected by this error.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-27002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The OpenSCAD converter timed out after <ADDITIONAL_DATA> seconds!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Scad/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An OpenSCAD render exceeded the time limit set by --SCAD Conversion Timeout-- in config.php and was terminated.
  OpenSCAD has no execution limit of its own and will render until it finishes or is killed.
  A complex assembly may legitimately need more time than the configured limit allows.
  A crafted model can pin a CPU core indefinitely through recursion or an extreme $fn value.
  Renders are niced to the lowest priority, so a runaway model yields to everything else on the server.
  Raising this limit multiplies the cost of the cheapest denial of service available against this server.
  A three line file uploaded by a stranger can consume a PHP worker for the entire configured duration.
  A large real world assembly of roughly thirty sources renders well within the default.
  OpenSCAD builds newer than the pinned minimum render considerably faster because they use multiple CPU cores.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-27003, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The OpenSCAD converter failed with exit code <ADDITIONAL_DATA>!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Scad/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  OpenSCAD ran but exited with a failure status and produced no usable output.
  The source may contain a syntax error, or may reference a variable or module that does not exist.
  The source may reference an include which HRConvert2 removed, leaving the remaining code unable to render.
  The source may reference a file outside the sandbox, which does not exist from inside it.
  A reference to any path on the server other than the session directory will fail this way & that is intended.
  The requested export format may be one this OpenSCAD build was not compiled to support.
  The source may produce two dimensional geometry, which cannot be exported to a three dimensional format.
  OpenSCAD standard output and standard error are deliberately discarded and never written to the log.
  A failed parse quotes the offending line, which would turn the log into a file exfiltration channel.
  Only the exit code is recorded, because an exit code cannot carry the contents of a file.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-27004, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not remove the sanitized OpenSCAD sources!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Scad/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The sanitized copies written to the ScadTemp directory could not be removed after the render finished.
  Sanitized sources are never retained for any reason, and are regenerated on every conversion if needed again.
  The www-data:www-data user:group may not have write access to the user session directory.
  A sanitized copy may be locked by another process, such as an OpenSCAD render that has not fully exited.
  Any copies left behind are removed by the session sweep once the --File Deletion Age Threshold-- has elapsed.
  The ScadTemp directory lives inside the non-hosted user session directory and is never reachable from the web.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-27005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed OpenSCAD version is missing or too old!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Scad/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The OpenSCAD dependency required to render OpenSCAD sources could not be verified.
  OpenSCAD may not be installed, or may not be reachable on the system path used by the web server user.
  The installed OpenSCAD may be older than the minimum Resources/Contract/depends.php gives it.
  HRConvert2 does not probe OpenSCAD for capabilities and does not accommodate older builds.
  Pinning a minimum version means the export formats offered in the interface can be trusted as written.
  Verify the installed version by running 'openscad --version' as the web server user.
  OpenSCAD reports its version on standard error rather than standard output.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-27006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not read the OpenSCAD source file <FILE_NAME>!

FILE

  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An uploaded OpenSCAD source could not be read while sanitizing the upload set.
  The file may have been deleted by the user or by the session sweep between upload and conversion.
  The www-data:www-data user:group may not have read access to the user session directory.
  The file may be larger than the memory limit PHP is configured to allow.
  Every uploaded OpenSCAD source is read during sanitization, not only the one selected for conversion.
  A single unreadable source therefore fails the whole conversion, because a resolved include could have pointed at it.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-27007, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so OpenSCAD renders cannot be isolated!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Scad/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  HRConvert2 could not create the sandbox that every OpenSCAD render is required to run inside.
  OpenSCAD reads any file the web server user can read & provides no sandbox of its own.
  It also cannot be given one through its arguments, the way FFMPEG accepts a protocol whitelist.
  HRConvert2 previously filtered the source instead & that approach was reported as bypassable.
  A filter that inspects one line at a time cannot agree with a parser that carries state across lines.
  A block comment closed part way through a line is inert to the filter & live code to OpenSCAD.
  A statement whose name & opening bracket sit on different lines matches no filter pattern at all.
  Both were confirmed working bypasses, so filtering is no longer treated as a boundary.
  Scad conversions are now refused outright when the sandbox cannot be created.
  There is deliberately no fallback to filtering alone, because filtering alone does not hold.
  Install bubblewrap on the server with 'apt install bubblewrap' to resolve this error.
  Bubblewrap may also be installed but non functional on this system.
  Unprivileged user namespaces can be disabled at the kernel level on some hardened systems.
  Check the setting by running 'sysctl kernel.unprivileged_userns_clone' on the server.
  On Ubuntu 23.10 & later the AppArmor restriction applies too, & -v says whether every sandbox
  runs HRConvert2's own copy of bubblewrap, which the hrconvert2-bwrap profile -fp writes is
  attached to. --setup --update-depends installs the copy.
  Some container runtimes also block the namespaces bubblewrap requires.
  Running HRConvert2 inside Docker may require adding the SYS_ADMIN capability, or a seccomp profile that permits unshare.
  Verify the sandbox works by running the following as the web server user, which must exit silently.
  bwrap --unshare-all --ro-bind /usr /usr --proc /proc --dev /dev --tmpfs /tmp /usr/bin/true
  Where the kernel restricts unprivileged namespaces, run /usr/local/libexec/hrconvert2/bwrap in place of bwrap.
  Set the Allowed Conversion Types configuration entry to exclude Scad if this server cannot support the sandbox.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-27008, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not move the rendered OpenSCAD model out of the sandbox!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Scad/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The render completed & produced a file, but that file could not be moved to its final location.
  The sandbox cannot see the real output location, so every render writes inside the session ScadTemp directory first.
  The finished model is then moved out from outside the sandbox, where the real location is visible.
  The www-data:www-data user:group needs write access to the user session directory.
  This may be the result of incorrect file permission levels on the data location.
  The disk or the partition holding the data storage directory may be full.
  A file may already exist at the destination & may be locked by another process.
  The rendered model is left inside the ScadTemp directory when this happens.
  It is removed by the sanitized source cleanup that runs immediately afterwards.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-27009, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A file reference survived sanitization of the OpenSCAD source <FILE>!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Scad/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Every uploaded OpenSCAD source is rewritten to remove the calls that read files, & the
  rewritten source is handed back to the Sanitizer before it is staged. This error means the
  Sanitizer could still see a call in the rewritten source, so the rewrite did not do its job.
  Nothing was staged & nothing was rendered from that source. The conversion is refused.
  This is a refusal rather than a warning on purpose. A source that still carries a live file
  reference is the exact condition the Sanitizer exists to prevent, & rendering it anyway
  would mean trusting the rewrite rather than checking it.
  An operator seeing this should keep the source & report it, because a source that reaches
  this point is either a new evasion shape or a defect in the rewrite.
  Documentation/Build/hrconvert2-test-scad-cli.php exercises both halves & is the first place
  to reproduce it.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-28000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The config.php file is missing <ADDITIONAL_DATA> required setting(s). Config version detected: <ADDITIONAL_DATA>. Config version required: <ADDITIONAL_DATA>. Missing Variables: <ADDITIONAL_DATA>

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  config.php does not carry every setting this core requires & the count of missing ones is
  in the message.
  An undefined setting reads as NULL in PHP, which silently becomes FALSE or zero at every
  point of use, so a missing setting is a silently changed behaviour rather than an error.
  Run --config --repair as root. It adds every missing setting at its shipped default,
  inside the section that setting belongs to, & touches no existing value but the version
  stamp, which it raises to the version the core requires once every setting is present.
  A backup is written before anything is changed & the file is checked with php -l before
  it replaces the original, so a repair that would not parse never lands.

    sudo php convertCore.php --config --repair

  The version stamp is reported & never enforced by that utility. An out of date file is
  exactly the file somebody has opened it to fix.
  This almost always means convertCore.php was updated & config.php was not.
  The list of missing variables in this message is complete & names every setting that was absent.
  A config file that predates the version stamp entirely reports a detected version of NONE.
  A config file that carries every required setting is accepted regardless of what its stamp says.
  The settings config.php must hold are the ones the two halves of the configuration template hold,
  Resources/Engine/config-template.php & Resources/Contract/config-template.php. The kernel reads their
  names & makes each a global as config.php is read, so a setting is required from the release that
  adds it to either half, & there is no separate list of them to fall out of step.
  Until v4.2.4 this could also appear when a setting was in config.php & the required list but not in the
  global declaration where config.php was read, & that declaration no longer exists.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-28001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The core file reports version <ADDITIONAL_DATA> but the version information file reports version <ADDITIONAL_DATA>. This installation is incomplete or was updated incorrectly.

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  convertCore.php & Resources/versionInfo.php disagree about which version this is.
  An update was interrupted, or one file was replaced by hand & the other was not.
  Running in this state would judge every component against a version that is not the one
  actually installed.
  Complete the update, or replace both files from the same release.
  The two files are checked against each other before anything else is verified, because every
  later check assumes the core & the release it belongs to are actually the same release.
  Keep config.php & the secret when reinstalling the complete release over the installation.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-28005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The Engine is unavailable, so this request cannot be served. Run  php <ENTRY_POINT> -v  on the server to see which component failed.

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A web request arrived & Resources/Engine/engine.php is missing, unreadable, or reports a version this
  core does not require. From v4.3.1 an Engine that loaded & did not start is unavailable too.
  Conversion, resource budgeting, data location cleaning, URL fetching & stream handling all live in the
  Engine, so there is nothing a visitor could successfully do on this installation.
  This halt exists so the failure is a sentence rather than a blank page. Before it was added, the first
  unguarded Engine call produced an undefined function & the visitor saw nothing at all.
  The command line is deliberately NOT halted here. Run  php <ENTRY_POINT> -v  to see which component
  failed & what version it reports. Without an Engine, -h lists only -v & -h & says why.
  An Engine refused on version grounds is the common cause & the fix is to install the matching Engine
  rather than to edit either version stamp.
  A request that arrives the moment an update is unpacked can meet it too. PHP's opcode cache looks at a
  file for changes at most every opcache.revalidate_freq seconds, two by default, so for that long it can
  run the core it cached against an Engine on disk that is already the new one. Requests after that are
  served by the new release, & nothing needs repairing.
  The cache knows a file has changed only by its modification time. A drop in unpacked without --touch
  gives each file the time the drop in was built with, & a file whose time matches the one it replaced
  is never read again, so this error stays until the web server is reloaded.

    sudo systemctl reload apache2
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <APPLICATION_NAME>-28006, The kernel is missing or unreadable at <PATH>! Nothing can be loaded
  or logged without it.

FILE
  convertCore.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  NO

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php is absent, or the web server cannot read it.
  The kernel holds logging, the halt function & the code that loads every other component, the Engine
  included, so this is the one failure that cannot be logged & cannot fall back to anything. It is a plain
  die for that reason, & it is printed rather than written to a log that does not exist yet.
  The kernel is found beside convertCore.php rather than through the configured installation directory,
  because the configuration has not been read when it is needed.
  Restore Resources/Kernel/kernel.php from the release this core came from.
  A drop-in carries only the files its release changed. Unpacked over the hosted location, it leaves a full
  convertCore.php there with no kernel beside it. Unpack a drop-in into the installation, the Application
  folder in the data location, & run the -fp argument from there. That -fp publishes the loader again.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <APPLICATION_NAME>-28007, The kernel at <PATH> reports <VERSION> & this application requires
  <VERSION>!

FILE
  convertCore.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  NO

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php is present but is not the version this core was built against.
  The version is read from the file WITHOUT executing it, so a mismatched kernel never runs. The same
  guarantee a mismatched Engine gets.
  The common cause is a partial update, or a kernel copied in from a different application or release.
  Install the kernel from the release this core came from. Do not edit either version stamp to make them
  agree, because the stamps are the only thing that says the code underneath them matches.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-28008, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Root refused to run the installation at <PATH>, because an account other than root could have changed it. <ADDITIONAL_DATA> Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp  to give it back to root. Where -fp says root still may not run it, remove or put right what it names. Any other account may still run -v in the meantime.

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  NO

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A command run as root found something in the installation, or above it, that an account other
  than root could change, & stopped before running anything from it but the kernel.
  Root runs nothing another account can change. Every folder from / down to the installation must
  belong to root & be writable by nobody else. Every name inside the installation must belong to
  root & be writable by nobody else, & no name may be a link. DATA is never examined.
  The message names the first thing that failed.
  An installation left by v4.0.0 belongs to the web server account. Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp
  once & it is root's. -fp, --setup & --config --repair run on such an installation after a warning,
  because they are what give it to root.
  A file someone made group writable or gave to another account is corrected by -fp as well.
  A link or a file with more than one name inside the installation is left where it is by -fp, &
  -fp says so as it ends. Remove it by hand & run -fp again.
  What an earlier release left in the installation & no release ships is the exception. The
  contract names each such path, & -fp removes it once the rest of the installation is root's.
  The FFmpeg build until v4.0.6 left its whole tree in Documentation/Build/ffmpeg-build, links
  included, which is why the v4.0.5 image refused to run with this number. -fp from v4.0.6
  removes it.
  A folder above the installation, such as /var/www/html, is not this application's & -fp leaves it
  alone. A web server account that owns it can swap the whole installation for its own. Confirm
  nothing else on the server relies on writing there, then give it to root.
    sudo chown root:root /var/www/html
    sudo chmod 0755 /var/www/html
  Since v4.0.1 the installation is Application in the data location, & root also reads the hosted
  location, --Installation Directory--, whenever it runs the loader there, because the loader ran
  first. -fp gives the hosted location's own files back to root.
  The loader refuses before it hands anything over, with this number & a shorter sentence, when the
  installation or its folder could have been changed by another account. Run -fp from the
  installation itself then, as the sentence says, because the loader refuses it too.
  Every account but root is unaffected. The message is not logged, because it is raised before a
  logfile exists, & it is printed instead.
  See Documentation/ABOUT_MANAGED_LOCATIONS.txt.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-28009, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The configuration template at <PATH> is missing or unreadable, so the settings config.php must hold are unknown! Restore it from the release this installation came from.

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  config.php has two halves, & each has a template. The Engine's is Resources/Engine/config-template.php &
  the application's is Resources/Contract/config-template.php.
  The kernel reads the names of the settings in both as every request & command starts, to know which settings
  config.php must hold & which of them become globals. A half it cannot read leaves every one of its settings
  unread, so each would read as NULL wherever it is used, which is a silently changed behaviour rather than an error.
  So nothing runs, not even --config --repair, which writes a configuration from the same two files.
  Restore the file named from the release this installation came from. A drop-in from v4.2.4 onward carries both.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-28010, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The account running this process could not be identified, because posix_geteuid is unavailable & /proc/self/status could not be read. It may be root, so nothing was run. Enable the posix extension in PHP.

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  NO

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Every decision the kernel makes about root rests on the ID of the account running the process, & this one
  could not be read. posix_geteuid names it, & /proc/self/status does where PHP has no posix functions.
  Neither answered, so the process may be root, & it stops before anything in the installation runs rather
  than guess. A web request stops the same way.
  Distribution PHP carries the posix extension. This is a server where an administrator disabled it, or put
  posix_geteuid in disable_functions, & also kept PHP from reading /proc, as open_basedir can. Enable the
  extension, or take posix_geteuid out of disable_functions.
  Until v4.3.3 the kernel asked whoami instead, which is another program, found through PATH.
  The loader in the hosted location refuses with this number & a shorter sentence on the command line, & a
  browser is told only that the application is unavailable.
  The message is not logged, because it is raised before a logfile exists, & it is printed instead.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not determine the latest release from the update source!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/environmentCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  HRConvert2 asked the update source which release is newest & could not read an answer.
  This only occurs when the update target is 'latest'. A pinned version needs no such request.
  The --Automatic Update Target Version-- entry in config.php controls which target is used.
  The server may have no outbound network access, which is a deliberate configuration on many
  installations & means automatic updates are not available on this server.
  The update source may be unreachable, rate limited, or temporarily unavailable.
  The GitHub API permits sixty unauthenticated requests per hour from one address.
  The --Update Source Repository-- entry in config.php may name a repository that does not
  exist, is private, or has no published releases at all.
  A repository with tags but no releases will fail this check, because a tag & a release are
  not the same thing to the update source.
  Pin an exact version in config.php or supply one on the command line to bypass this request.
  Update HRConvert2 by hand if this server cannot reach the update source at all.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The requested update target is not a recognized version!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/environmentCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The update target supplied is neither a recognized keyword nor a valid version number.
  Three targets are accepted & nothing else is.
  'latest' installs the newest tagged release.
  'edge' installs the current state of the master branch, which is not a release & carries
  whatever version stamp master happens to hold.
  A version of the form v3.6.8 installs exactly that tag.
  The version pattern is strict. Three numbers separated by dots, with an optional leading v.
  A partial version such as v3.6 is refused, because it names a series rather than a release.
  A target is read from the command line first & from --Automatic Update Target Version--
  in config.php when none was supplied.
  The target is refused BEFORE any network request is made, because it would otherwise be
  placed directly into a URL.
  Supply the target as  -u=v3.6.8  or as  -u v3.6.8  on the command line.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The requested update version does not exist at the update source!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The update source answered, & said there is no such release.
  Curl exited with code 22, which is an HTTP error status rather than a transport failure.
  A version was requested that has never been tagged, or was tagged & later removed.
  The version may be spelled differently at the source. v3.6.8 & 3.6.8 are the same to
  HRConvert2 but a tag named 3.6.8 without the v will not be found, because the URL is
  always built with the v.
  The --Update Source Repository-- entry in config.php may name the wrong repository.
  A private repository answers this way to an unauthenticated request, whether or not the
  version exists. HRConvert2 does not authenticate to the update source.
  Nothing was changed. The installation is untouched.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29003, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The update package is larger than the configured maximum!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The download was refused because it exceeded --Maximum Update Package Size-- in config.php.
  The default is one hundred megabytes, which is far larger than any legitimate release.
  A release that has genuinely grown past the limit needs the limit raised.
  A release that has not should be treated as suspicious. An update package is application
  code & is the highest value target in the system, so an unexpectedly large one is refused
  rather than trusted.
  The limit exists so a hostile or misconfigured source cannot fill the disk.
  Nothing was changed. The installation is untouched.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29004, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not download the update package! Curl exited with code <ADDITIONAL_DATA>.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The download failed for a reason other than a missing version or an oversized package.
  The curl exit code identifies the cause & is recorded in the message.
  Code 6 means the update source hostname could not be resolved. Check DNS.
  Code 7 means the connection was refused. Check outbound network access & any firewall.
  Code 28 means the transfer exceeded --Update Connection Timeout-- in config.php.
  Code 35 or 60 means a TLS failure. The certificate store may be out of date.
  Code 127 means curl is not installed. It is required for updates & for stream inspection.
  Many installations deliberately have no outbound network access, which is a reasonable
  configuration & means automatic updates are not available on that server.
  Nothing was changed. The installation is untouched.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The downloaded update package is empty or too small to be valid!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The download reported success but produced a file under one kilobyte.
  A release archive is never that small, so whatever arrived is not a release.
  A proxy or captive portal may have answered instead of the update source.
  The disk holding the temporary directory may be full.
  This check exists because a zero byte file extracts to nothing & would otherwise be
  treated as a valid but empty release.
  Nothing was changed. The installation is untouched.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not read a configuration file during the merge!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Either the live configuration or the one in the downloaded release could not be read.
  The live file is at Resources/config.php inside the installation directory.
  The www-data:www-data user:group needs read access to both.
  The downloaded release may be incomplete, or may not contain a configuration file at all.
  Nothing was changed. The installation is untouched.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29007, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not merge the configuration file!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The merge itself failed rather than the reading or the writing.
  The new configuration file provides the structure & the comments. The live file provides
  the values for every setting that exists in both. A setting that is new keeps its default,
  & a setting that was removed is discarded with the old file.
  A failure here means the pattern match over the new file did not complete, which usually
  means the file is not valid PHP or is not the file HRConvert2 expected to find.
  Nothing was changed. The installation is untouched.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29009, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Automatic updates are disabled in config.php!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An update was requested on a server where --Enable Automatic Updates-- is FALSE.
  This is the default. A server does not gain the ability to replace its own code unless an
  administrator has deliberately granted it.
  Set $EnableAutoUpdates to TRUE to permit updates from the command line, with
  sudo php convertCore.php --config  & then  set EnableAutoUpdates  in General Information,
  or in config.php itself. The refusal names this installation's own paths for both.
  An update is NEVER reachable from the web interface & never will be. Replacing application
  code requires write access to the installation directory, which is what shell access
  already implies & is the correct authorization for the operation.
  Nothing was changed. The installation is untouched.
