----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29014, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not stage the update beside the installation!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The prepared release could not be moved next to the installation directory.
  It has to be there rather than in the temporary directory, because the swap that follows
  uses rename(), & rename() is only atomic within one filesystem.
  The staged copy is written to the parent of the installation directory.
  The update runs as root, & that parent must belong to root & be writable by nobody else.
  ERROR 31023 refuses the update before this point when it is not.
  The parent may be on a read only mount, or carry an immutable attribute.
  The disk holding the installation may be full. A staged release is a full second copy of
  the application, so roughly twice the installed size must be free.
  Nothing was changed. The installation is untouched.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29015, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not move the existing installation aside!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The first of the two renames that perform the swap failed.
  The installation is replaced by exchanging directories rather than by overwriting files,
  so that there is never a moment where the installation is half of one version & half of
  another. This is the first half of that exchange & it did not happen.
  The parent of the installation may be on a read only mount, or carry an immutable attribute.
  The installation may be a mount point, which rename() cannot move.
  Nothing was changed. The installation is untouched & is still the version it was.
  The staged release is left behind & is removed at the start of the next update.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29016, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not move the update into place! The previous installation was restored.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The second of the two renames failed after the first had already succeeded.
  For a moment there was no installation directory at all. HRConvert2 detected this
  immediately & renamed the previous installation back into place before doing anything else.
  The previous version is running. Confirm this with  php convertCore.php -v
  A concurrent web request during that moment would have failed. It will not fail again.
  This is close to impossible, because the first rename proved the parent directory is
  writable & the second rename uses the same directory.
  A disk that filled between the two renames, or a filesystem remounted read only mid
  operation, would produce it.
  Verify the installation with  php convertCore.php -v  before attempting another update.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29017, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The updated installation failed validation & was rolled back!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The new version was installed, could not prove it runs, & the previous one was restored.
  Validation runs the newly installed core as a separate process & asks it to report its own
  version. A core that cannot parse, cannot load its configuration, or cannot reach the
  command line branch will not answer, & that is the condition this catches.
  A file count or a directory listing would prove nothing about whether the code executes.
  The previous version is running & the installation is intact.
  Confirm with  php convertCore.php -v
  The most likely cause is a configuration merge that produced a file the new core rejects,
  which happens when a setting changed meaning rather than merely changing name.
  The second most likely cause is a release that is genuinely broken.
  The WARNING entries above this error record what was attempted.
  Pin the previous version in config.php if the newest release cannot be installed.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29018, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The updated installation failed validation & COULD NOT be rolled back!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  THIS IS THE WORST OUTCOME THIS PROCESS CAN PRODUCE & REQUIRES MANUAL INTERVENTION.
  The new version failed validation, & the attempt to restore the previous version also
  failed. The installation directory may be absent, incomplete, or a mixture.
  The application is not serving requests correctly & will not until this is repaired.
  The previous installation is at the installation path with .old appended, unless the
  rename that was attempted partially succeeded.
  Restore it by hand.
    Stop the web server.
    Move or delete whatever is at the installation path.
    Rename the .old directory back to the installation path.
    Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp  so every location gets its role back.
    The code is root's & the web server user only reads it. Do not give it to www-data.
    Start the web server & confirm with  php convertCore.php -v
  The DATA directory holding live user sessions was moved between the two installations
  during the update. It is in whichever of the two directories still exists.
  The configuration file is in both. The one in the .old directory is the one that worked.
  A backup of the previous installation may also exist at the --Backup Location-- from an
  earlier successful update. That backup is one version older still.
  This error should not be possible. Something is seriously wrong with the filesystem.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-29019, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not move the DATA folder into the update, so the update was stopped & nothing was changed!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The DATA folder holding live user sessions could not be moved into the staged release.
  DATA is moved rather than copied, so a session that is open during the update survives it.
  Until v4.0.1 this move failed in silence on every update. The release ships a DATA folder of
  its own & a folder cannot be renamed over one that is not empty, so the live DATA stayed with
  the previous installation & was deleted with it. The release's own copy is removed first now,
  & an update that still cannot move DATA stops here rather than going on without it.
  Nothing was changed. The installation & its DATA are where they were.
  DATA may be a mount point, which rename() cannot move. Unmount it before updating & mount it
  again afterwards.
  DATA may carry an immutable attribute, or sit on a read only mount.
  The staged release is left behind & is removed at the start of the next update.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-30000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The e-book converter produced no file after <ADDITIONAL_DATA> attempt(s)!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Ebook/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Calibre ran & left no file. From v4.3.1 it is asked again only where a signal stopped it,
  as many times as --File Operation Retry Count-- allows. Until then it was asked that many times whatever happened.
  Until v4.0.6 this read The e-book converter timed out! No time limit ever caused it, & one more attempt was made after it.
  With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the entry immediately above this one carries what Calibre printed, which usually names the cause.
  A book Calibre cannot read is the usual cause. A format it cannot write it names as No plugin to handle output format.
  Until v4.0.6 pml & oeb were offered. Calibre writes PML only zipped, as pmlz, & OEB as a folder, so neither ever reached a user.
  A converter its memory ceiling killed ends the same way. With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the Conversion Limit entry names that ceiling, & --Maximum Per Conversion Resources-- in config.php sets the most each type may be given.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-30002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so this e-book conversion cannot be isolated!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Ebook/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The sandbox could not be prepared, & an e-book is never converted without one.
  An e-book is an archive of documents & scripts written by somebody else, so a converter

  run unprotected.
  Calibre copies into the book it writes any file an EPUB's manifest names, wherever it is on

  RequireSandbox, so --Require Sandbox-- & --Require Sandbox On Docker-- do not decide this.
  Until then setting the one that applies to FALSE allowed this conversion, & was not advised.

ERROR MESSAGE

FILE

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The Core Manager process could not bind a unix domain socket in the socket directory.
  The socket directory does not exist or is not writable by the web server user.
  A previous listener left a socket file behind that could not be removed.
  The socket directory lives inside the data location & is named by the --status argument.
  Run the -fp argument as root to correct ownership & permissions on every managed path.
  This error only occurs if --Enable Resource Awareness-- is set to TRUE in config.php.
  This error halts the Core Manager process only. Conversions continue without resource awareness.

  YES

  Run the -fp argument as root to correct ownership & permissions on every managed path.

  This error halts the Request Manager process only. The Core Manager will restart it.

  The Sockets directory inside the data location could not be created or is not writable.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The Core Manager listener could not be started!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/engine.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The Core Manager process was launched but did not record itself within five seconds.
  The launched process died before it validated its startup key.
  The web server user cannot read the install secret, so no valid startup key could be derived.
  The web server user cannot write the logfile, so the process failed silently.
  PHP is not on the PATH of the account the listener was launched under.
  The su command is unavailable, which is required when root starts the listener.
  The command needed to reproduce the failure by hand is printed when this error occurs.
  Run that command without the output redirection to see why the process is dying.
  This error only occurs when the -l or --listen argument is used.
  This error is not fatal. The listener simply does not start.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A manager process was started with an invalid startup key!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/engine.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A manager process was launched without a key derived from the install secret.
  A command line user attempted to start a listener component by hand.
  The supplied key was derived from a per user secret rather than the install wide secret.
  The supplied key was derived more than two key windows ago & has expired.
  The install secret was rotated between the launch & the validation.
  The key was presented once already & refused as a replay, which ERROR 31017 just above this one says.
  Before v4.1.9 a listener stopped & started again inside ten seconds was refused this way, & from v4.1.9 every launch mints a key of its own.
  The application is being fuzzed or scanned for vulnerabilities.
  The --start-core-manager & --start-manager arguments are internal & are not documented in -h.
  They are invoked by the -l argument & are refused without a valid key.
  A standard user cannot start a valid listener, because a per user secret cannot derive this key.

  YES

  The --start-manager argument was supplied with a role that does not exist.
  The four valid roles are core-manager, request-manager, resource-manager & worker-manager.
  The Core Manager component & convertCore.php are from different releases.
  A command line user attempted to start a listener component by hand.
  This error is in place to maintain syntactical integrity of the codebase.
  Logically this error should never be triggered, because the role is supplied by the Core Manager
  itself & the startup key would already have been refused for any other caller.
  The second message means the role is real & its manager is missing, or is not the version the
  Engine pins. A WARNING before it says which. Put Resources/Engine back from the release the
  application came from, & start the listener again.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31013, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Resource awareness is required by config.php but --Enable Resource Awareness-- is FALSE!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31013, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Resource awareness is required by config.php but there is no install secret!

  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  --Require Resource Awareness-- is TRUE while --Enable Resource Awareness-- is FALSE.
  These two settings contradict each other & the application refuses rather than guessing which was meant.
  Set --Enable Resource Awareness-- to TRUE, or set --Require Resource Awareness-- to FALSE.
  The configuration utility is let through, so  sudo php convertCore.php --config  shows the contradiction & can repair it.
  The second wording means no install secret is available, so resource awareness could not work even if it were enabled.
  A secret is unavailable when the secret file belongs to another account, which -fp corrects.
  ERROR 31010 is raised when the Engine itself did not load.
  This error halts the application, because the administrator has declared that this server must
  never accept work it cannot account for.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31014, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The foreground listener was started by an account that may not operate it!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/engine.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The --run-core-manager argument was used by an account other than the web server user.
  Root is refused too. The listener sweeps the web server user's folders & keeps its state
  there, so it only ever runs as that user. Start it with -l, which hands it to that user.
  A command line user attempted to start the listener by hand.
  A service unit was installed with the wrong User setting.
  --run-core-manager is the entry point a service manager uses. It does not fork & does not return.
  A standard user holds a per user secret, which cannot derive a valid startup key, so a listener
  started that way could never be reached by any worker even if this check permitted it.
  Correct the User setting in the unit, or start the listener with the -l argument instead.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31015, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The foreground listener could not derive a startup key. No install secret is available!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/engine.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The install secret could not be read by the account the listener is running as.
  The secret file is root's & the web server user reads it through its group, root:www-data 0640.
  A file left by a release before v4.0.1 is not in that shape until a command runs as root.
  The secret file was removed or rotated while the listener was starting.
  The listener is running as an account that resolves a per user secret rather than the install secret.
  Run the -fp argument as root, which corrects the ownership of the secret file.
  A listener without the install secret cannot derive the keys its sockets are encrypted with, so
  every message a worker sent would be discarded as undecryptable & the listener would answer nobody.

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The --run-core-manager argument was used while the Core Manager component was missing or invalid.
  The coreManager.php file is absent, unreadable, or reports no version.
  The component version does not exactly match the version this core requires.
  --Enable Resource Awareness-- is set to FALSE in config.php.
  A service unit will restart the process & raise this again until the component is corrected.
  Disable the hrconvert2-listener unit while the component is unavailable, or repair the component.
  ERROR 31009 & ERROR 31012 are the same condition raised for the two internal start arguments.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31017, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A startup key for <ADDITIONAL_DATA> was presented a second time & refused as a replay!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/engine.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A manager startup key that had already been spent was presented again.
  A startup key is a launch number drawn at random and an HMAC over it and a short time bucket, and is valid for the current window and the one before it.
  From v4.1.9 every launch draws a launch number of its own, so two launches of one manager role inside one window mint two keys.
  A key that crosses a process boundary is now spent the moment it validates, and a second process presenting the same key is refused however soon it happens.
  Time alone cannot make a leaked key dead. Whoever holds a copy validates it against the same clock this application does, so the window decides only how long they have and never whether they succeed. Spending the key is the part this application controls.
  The key is also carried in the environment rather than on the command line, because /proc/PID/cmdline is world readable and a manager process runs for days.
  CAUSE. A replay of a captured key, so a run of these with no matching launch is worth investigating.
  Before v4.1.9 a key carried no launch number, & a listener stopped & started again inside ten seconds was refused this way.
  A listener started by a release before v4.1.9 & still running after an update mints such keys until it is restarted.
  So does a host that could not supply random bytes, which a WARNING says. See Warnings About Startup Keys below.
  RESOLUTION. Run --status to see what is already running. A listener started by an earlier release is restarted with -k & -l.
  The ledger of spent keys lives in the manager socket directory and holds hashes rather than keys, so reading it grants nothing. Entries older than three windows are dropped automatically.
  This control defends against a key that leaked, not against an account that holds the install secret.
  Deleting the ledger un-spends a captured key, and the only accounts that can delete it are root and the web server user, both of which can already read the secret and mint a valid key directly. Losing the ledger to either of them costs nothing that was not already lost.
  The directory holding it sits inside the data location, owned by the web server user, so an unprivileged local account cannot remove it. That is the account this control exists for.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WARNINGS ABOUT STARTUP KEYS
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  The Engine raises these, from Resources/Engine/engine.php.
    WARNING!!! A startup key for <PURPOSE> was refused.
      The key did not validate for its purpose, in this window or the one before it. A manager that presented it does not
      start, & ERROR 31006 follows. A key spent once already is refused with ERROR 31017 instead, & not with this warning.
    WARNING!!! A startup key for <PURPOSE> carries no launch number, because this host could not supply random bytes. A second launch inside the same window will be refused as a replay.
      From v4.1.9. PHP's random_bytes failed, so the key was minted as a release before v4.1.9 mints one, a signature alone.
      It still validates. A second launch of the same purpose inside the same ten second window mints the same key, & that
      one is refused with ERROR 31017. A host without a working source of random bytes is also a host where a session token
      cannot be issued, so the cause is worth correcting.
    WARNING!!! The ledger of spent startup keys at <FILE_PATH> could not be opened & locked, so a startup key for <PURPOSE> was accepted without being recorded as spent.
      From v4.1.9. The key validated, & is accepted, because a manager that could not start over its ledger would take the
      listener down with it. It was not recorded, so it could be presented again until its window closes. The ledger is in the
      manager socket directory, which is the web server account's & 0700. Run -fp as root, which gives the folder back.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  resolved to a directory the operating system needs.
  NOTHING WAS DELETED. This is the guard working, not a failure of the update.
  Correct $InstLoc in config.php & run the update again. The path is resolved through
  symlinks & .. before it is judged, so check what it actually points at rather than what
  it looks like.

  operating system needs.

  An empty $BackupLoc is fine & means backups are off. A wrong one is not.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31020, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: An install secret could not be generated, because this host could not supply random bytes!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31020, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The install secret could not be written to <FILE_PATH>! <ADDITIONAL_DATA> It is the copy the web server account reads first, & it does not carry the key root holds.
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31020, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The install secret written to <FILE_PATH> did not read back correctly! It is the copy the web server account reads first, & it does not carry the key root holds.
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31020, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The install secret at <FILE_PATH> could not be reached, because another account could replace <FOLDER_PATH>, so nothing was replaced! <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31020, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The install secret was not rotated, because another account could replace <FOLDER_PATH>, & the copy there would keep the old key! <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31020, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The install secret was not written to <FILE_PATH>, because another account could replace it there. <ADDITIONAL_DATA> It is the copy the web server account reads first, & it does not carry the key root holds.
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31020, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: <WRITE_FAILURE> It is not known to carry the key in use & could not be removed either. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31020, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: <WRITE_FAILURE> It still carries the old key until the next command run as root writes the new one there.
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31020, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The web server account would not read the install secret root holds! <ADDITIONAL_DATA>

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Root could not put a usable install secret in place.
  Every command run as root reads the install secret, & writes it again when it is missing,
  unusable, or not yet in the shape this release keeps it in. That shape is root:www-data 0640,
  so the web server user can read the key & cannot change it. From v4.0.4 the same key is kept
  in two copies, in Secrets in the data location & at the top of the data location, where every
  earlier release reads it. The web server user reads the copy in Secrets first once the folder
  has its role, unless the copy at the top is root's & was written after it, & the copy at the top
  first until then. The path in the message says which copy.
  A copy read first that cannot be written is an ERROR when the key is new or a rotation's.
  Otherwise the key was found in a copy that still carries it, so it is only a WARNING, & the
  next command run as root tries again. The other copy that cannot be written is only a WARNING.
  One not known to carry the key in use is removed, so no other key can ever be found there,
  once the web server user can read the copy it reads first, & is an ERROR when it cannot be
  removed. While that copy is not right it is left, because that user may read it meanwhile.
  THE KEY IS IN USE ONLY WHEN THE WEB SERVER USER READS IT. Once the copies are settled, root
  judges from the owners, the groups & the modes which copy that user would use first, passing
  over one it cannot read & one it cannot use, as it does, & the key root holds is in use only
  when that copy carries it. The ninth message says when it does not, & names the copy that user
  would use, or says it could use neither.
  Root reads the file with PHP's tokenizer & never runs it. It writes a new copy that did not
  exist a moment before under a staged name of its own beside the secret's, gives it its group &
  mode, & renames it over the name, so nothing at either name is ever followed.
  The first message means random_bytes() failed. The host has no usable source of randomness.
  The second message carries the reason the write failed. The data location may be full, on a
  read only mount, or missing. The warnings above it say whether root could create it. The new
  copy may not have taken the name, which a file made immutable refuses, or its mode may not
  have been set. A copy a default access control list made wider than 0640, in a folder another
  account owns, is never given the key or the name, because each new copy is made empty & judged
  before the key is written into it, & only the file judged is filled. When the group cannot be
  given & the name holds a copy the web server user reads, that copy is kept rather than replaced
  by one it could not read. When the write fails, the secret in place is unchanged.
  The third message means the file on disk is not the file that was written. Something changed
  it in the moment between the write & the read. Once -fp has given each folder its role, only
  root can write where the secret is kept, so it was another command run as root, a folder
  somebody made writable since, or a disk that did not keep what it was given. The next command
  run as root examines it again & replaces it if it is still wrong.
  The fourth message means the copy in Secrets is out of root's reach, because a folder it is
  reached through could be replaced by another account. The data location is the usual one. A
  repair, & every command run as root once Secrets is in it, gives it back to root first, so
  this follows only when that could not be done, or when Secrets itself is a link. The copy may
  be the good one, so root makes no new key in its place & changes nothing. ADDITIONAL_DATA
  names what is in the way. Give that folder back to root, root:root 0755, & run
    sudo php convertCore.php -fp
  The fifth message is the same thing during --rotate-secret. The copy in Secrets cannot be
  written, so it would keep the old key & could be found again, & nothing is rotated.
  The sixth message means the place the secret is kept could be changed by an account other than
  its owner, through the folder named in ADDITIONAL_DATA or one above it. A group that may write
  counts whoever the group is, because under an access control list the group bits are only the
  list's mask. The web server user would refuse a copy there, & so would the next command run as
  root, so none is written. -fp gives the data location & Secrets back to root first. A folder
  above the data location is not this application's, & ERROR 31024 names it.
  WRITE_FAILURE in the seventh & eighth messages is the second, third or sixth message without its last
  sentence, about a copy other than the one read first. Until v4.3.1 those three ended without it.
  The seventh message means that copy is not known to carry the key in use & could not be removed
  either. ADDITIONAL_DATA says why. The web server user reads the key in the copy it reads first,
  so a key in the other copy is found only when that copy cannot be used. Remove the copy named as
  root, & run
    sudo php convertCore.php -fp
  The eighth message means --rotate-secret wrote the new key into the copy read first & not into
  the other, which still carries the old key. The copy read first was not given the web server
  user's group, so the other was not removed either. The WARNING above it says why. Until the
  next command run as root writes the new key there, the web server user may use the old one.
  The ninth message means the copy the web server user would use first does not carry the key
  root holds, or that user could use neither copy. A copy root:root 0640, a folder that user
  cannot enter, or a copy carrying another key that root could not write are the usual causes,
  & the WARNINGs above it say which copies could not be written. A file made immutable, a full
  disk or a read only mount keeps root from correcting them. Correct the cause & run -fp again.
  Without a key that user reads, the installation does not verify, so ERROR 5 follows & the
  command stops. Otherwise the command goes on with the key in use. A rotation is done once the
  copy read first carries the new key, & the ninth message still names a copy that user would
  read the old key from.
  Correct the cause & run  sudo php convertCore.php -v  to confirm.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31021, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: There is no install secret & the web server account does not create one. Run -fp as root to create it.

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A web request, or the web server user on the command line, found no install secret.
  Until v4.0.1 the web server user created the secret whenever it found none. It no longer does,
  because a secret the web server user could write is a secret root would one day have to read.
  Root creates it, root:www-data 0640, the first time any command runs as root.
  A fresh installation that has never run a command as root has no secret yet. The installer runs
  as root & creates it, & so does  sudo php convertCore.php -fp
  The data location may have been emptied or replaced by hand since the last command run as root.
  Secrets may have lost its role, root:www-data 0750, so the web server user cannot enter it,
  while the copy at the top of the data location is gone too. -fp gives the folder its role again
  & writes both copies.
  The message names no path, because it can reach a visitor's page.
  Without a secret the installation does not verify, so ERROR 5 follows & the page is not served.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31022, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The install secret could not be rotated! The secret in place is unchanged.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/engine.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The --rotate-secret argument was confirmed & a new secret could not be put in place.
  There are two causes, & the lines just above this one say which.
  A running listener could not be stopped first. The secret is never rotated past a listener
  that would keep answering with the old key, so nothing was changed & a WARNING says so. Stop
  it with  sudo php convertCore.php -k  & run the rotation again.
  ERROR 31020 just above this one says the new secret could not be written. The secret in place
  is unchanged, so every session continues to work. When ERROR 31020 reports a read back failure
  instead, the file on disk is not the one written, & the next command run as root examines it
  again. A listener that was stopped for the rotation is started again.
  Correct the cause & run  sudo php convertCore.php --rotate-secret  again.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31023, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The update was refused, because the folder it would happen in can be changed by an account other than root. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An update swaps the installation for the new release by renaming both, inside the folder that
  holds the installation, & root then runs the new release's code.
  An account that can write that folder can rename the installation aside between the two & put
  its own in its place, & root would run it. So the update refuses unless every folder from /
  down to the installation's parent, & the --Proprietary Directory--, belongs to root & can be
  written by nobody else.
  The message names the first folder that failed.
  When it names the --Proprietary Directory-- itself, run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp  & try
  again. -fp gives that folder to root when the folder above it is root's.
  Error 28008 usually answers first, because every command run as root reads every folder above
  the installation before it runs. This one still covers the --Proprietary Directory-- when it is
  configured somewhere that is not above the installation.
  When it names a folder above that, such as /var/www/html, -fp leaves it alone, because it is
  not this application's & other software may share it. A web server user that owns the web root
  can replace anything served from it. Confirm nothing else on the server relies on writing there,
  then give it to root.
    sudo chown root:root /var/www/html
    sudo chmod 0755 /var/www/html
  Nothing was downloaded & nothing was changed.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31024, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Root will not work beneath <FOLDERS>, because another account can change them. Give each back to root & run the command again.

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Every command run as root reads the install secret, which is in the data location, so every
  folder from / down to the folder above the data location must belong to root & be writable by
  nobody else. An account that can write one of them can rename the data location aside & put
  its own in its place, & root would then read & write wherever that led.
  So root stops before it touches the secret, -fp included, & names every folder that failed with
  its owner, group & mode. -fp corrects the locations it manages & never a folder above them.
  Releases before v4.0.1 caused this. v3.4.1 asked for /var/www to be given to the web server
  account, & older guides did the same to /DATA. Nothing needs that now, because the web server
  account's home is the Cache folder in the data location. The commands printed with the list
  give each folder back to root & change nothing inside it.
    sudo chown root:root /var/www /var/www/html /DATA
    sudo chmod 755 /var/www /var/www/html /DATA
  Run them for the folders named only, & without -R. Mode 755 keeps every account able to reach
  the folders below, which the web server account needs.
  A folder another account owns, such as a home folder, & a folder every account shares by
  design, such as /tmp, are named without commands. Move the location out of it instead.
  The folders above the hosted location & above each additional data location are named the
  same way when they fail, as a warning. Root goes on then & corrects nothing beneath them.
  Nothing was changed.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31025, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The web server account holds <IN_USE> of its <LIMIT> user namespaces! No conversion is granted until more than 1% are free.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Managers/resourceManager.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Every sandbox is a user namespace. The kernel refuses a new one once the web server account
  holds user.max_user_namespaces of them, & a conversion started then fails inside its sandbox.
  The Resource Manager counts that account's user namespaces every --Resource Poll Interval--.
  It warns when 10% of the limit or less is free. With 1% or less free it refuses every budget
  request, & each refused conversion is logged with the reason. Conversions are granted again at
  the first poll that finds more than 1% free. Nothing needs restarting.
  The count is a floor. A namespace with no process of its own in it is not counted.
  List what holds them. Each line is one namespace, its process count & its first process.
    sudo lsns -t user | grep www-data
  A conversion's sandbox ends with its conversion. A namespace another program of the web server
  account holds is that program's. End what is stuck, or end every tracked conversion with
    sudo php convertCore.php --kill-all-workers
  Where the limit is too low for this host, raise it well above what lsns counts, & keep it at
  boot. <NEW_LIMIT> is the number chosen.
    sudo sysctl -w user.max_user_namespaces=<NEW_LIMIT>
    echo 'user.max_user_namespaces = <NEW_LIMIT>' | sudo tee /etc/sysctl.d/60-user-namespaces.conf
  This error only occurs if --Enable Resource Awareness-- is set to TRUE in config.php & the
  listener is running.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-31026, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The disk holding <DATA_LOCATIONS> has <FREE> free, within its storage reserve of <RESERVE>! Nothing more is written to it until more is free.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Managers/resourceManager.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A disk holding a data location has --Storage Reserve-- megabytes free or less.
  The Resource Manager measures every disk holding a data location every --Resource Poll Interval--.
  While a disk is within the reserve, every upload & every operation that takes a budget is refused, & each
  refusal is logged with the reason. Downloads & deletes still work, because deleting is how space is freed.
  Work is accepted again at the first poll, or the first request, that finds more than the reserve free.
  Nothing needs restarting.
  It is logged once when the disk enters the reserve. A warning comes first, once a disk has
  --Low Storage Warning-- percent of its size free or less, unless the reserve is the larger of the two.
  See what is using the disk, largest first.
    sudo du -xh --max-depth=2 <DATA_LOCATION> | sort -rh | head -20
  Sessions older than --File Deletion Age Threshold-- are removed every --Storage Cleanup Interval--.
  A shorter threshold frees space sooner. Space held by anything else on the disk is the administrator's to free.
  This error only occurs if --Enable Resource Awareness-- is set to TRUE in config.php & the
  listener is running. It is never raised by -fp or by an installation, which measure nothing.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The destination named in --backup already exists.
  A backup NEVER overwrites. The whole purpose of the file is that it is the copy that survives,
  & silently replacing an earlier backup with a later one destroys the thing being protected.
  Choose a different destination, or move the existing file aside first.
  This error is not fatal. Nothing was copied & nothing was changed.

  A partial backup is left on disk. Delete it rather than trusting it.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-32005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The staged configuration does not parse & was discarded. <PARSER OUTPUT>

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The configuration this utility assembled was checked with php -l & was rejected.
  A value entered by hand contains a quote or a backslash that was not escaped.
  An array value was entered that is not valid PHP.
  The parser output is included in the message & names the line it objected to.
  This error is in place to maintain syntactical integrity of the codebase.
  Logically this error should be rare, because every value is formatted before it is written.
  This error is not fatal & is the check working. The existing configuration is untouched.
  There is no recovering from a syntax error in config.php from a web request, which is why
  the file is checked before it is installed rather than after.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-32009, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: This installation has no configuration file to operate on!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  No path was supplied & Resources/config.php does not exist in this installation.
  The installation is incomplete, or --Installation Directory-- does not describe this installation.
  A call arriving from convertCore.php never supplies a path, so it can only ever operate on the
  configuration this core is responsible for, & that file is not there.
  Reinstall config.php from the release that matches this core.
  This error is not fatal. Nothing was read & nothing was changed.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-32010, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Data locations can only be created while running as root!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A complete installation was attempted by an account other than root.
  Creating a data location outside the web root & handing it to the web server user requires root.
  Re-run the command with sudo.
  This error is not fatal. The installation stops at this stage & nothing after it is attempted.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-32011, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A required data location could not be created!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A directory named in config.php could not be created. The WARNING before it names the folder & why.
  Until v4.3.1 this file showed a path in the message, which the message has never carried.
  The parent directory does not exist & could not be created.
  The path names a mount point that is not mounted.
  The filesystem is read only or full.
  An entry in --Additional Data Locations-- names a path on a device that is not attached.
  verifyRequiredDirs deliberately REFUSES to create the data location at runtime & raises
  ERROR 1000 instead, because a data location that vanished is a mount that failed & creating
  an empty one on top of it would hide the fault. This error is the install time equivalent,
  where there is nothing to hide.
  This error is not fatal. The installation stops at this stage & nothing after it is attempted.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-32012, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A service unit can only be installed while running as root!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A service unit installation was attempted by an account other than root.
  Writing to /etc/systemd/system requires root.
  This is normally reached through the -fp argument, which is already refused to a standard user.
  Re-run the command with sudo.
  This error is not fatal. The listener can still be started with the -l argument.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-32013, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The listener service unit could not be written to <PATH>!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The unit file could not be written even though this invocation is running as root.
  /etc/systemd/system is read only, which is normal inside many containers.
  The filesystem is full.
  A unit that already matches what would be written is left completely alone & does not reach this.
  This error is not fatal. The listener can still be started with the -l argument, which is what
  a host without a running systemd does anyway.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-32014, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The web served DATA location could not be created at <PATH>!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  $ConvertTemp, which is <INSTALL_LOCATION>/DATA, could not be created during a setup run.
  This is the ONLY data location that lives inside the web root. Every other location the
  setup routine creates sits outside it & is reachable only by the application itself.
  The application copies a file here whenever a user asks to download or share it, so that a
  browser can fetch it by URL. Without this directory no download & no share link can work.
  The parent directory is not writable, or the filesystem is full or mounted read only.
  Setup continues. The core will create this directory again on the first request, but it
  will do so WITHOUT the protection file that makes the tree serve inert downloads, so run
  the -fp argument as root once the cause is corrected & read the exposure line it prints.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-32015, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The Environment Manager timer could not be written to <PATH>!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  One of the two unit files could not be written to /etc/systemd/system.
  The service & the timer are written together, so this reports either one failing.
  From v4.0.5 -fp writes them again wherever they are installed, so it can report this too.
  The usual cause is a read only /etc or a full disk. Neither is specific to this
  application, & the listener unit would fail the same way at the same moment.
  Nothing else is affected. The Environment Manager still runs by hand, as
  sudo php convertCore.php --run-environment-manager, & is simply not scheduled.
  See Documentation/ABOUT_ENVIRONMENT_MANAGER.txt.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-32016, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The Environment Manager timer was written & could not be enabled!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Both unit files exist & systemctl refused to enable the timer.
  The files are correct, so this is systemd declining rather than a fault in what was
  written. Read what it said & enable it by hand once the cause is dealt with.

    sudo systemctl enable --now hrconvert2-environment.timer

  A timer that is written & not enabled changes nothing. The manager is simply not
  scheduled, which is the same state a new installation is in.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DEPENDENCY STATUS
  misconfigured

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/dependencyCore.php

DESCRIPTION
  The manifest names a PATH for a dependency & nothing executable is at that path.
  This is not the same as a dependency being absent & is reported separately on purpose.
  An operator who named a path wants to know the path is wrong. Telling them to install a
  tool they already installed sends them the wrong way entirely.
  A Binary containing a path separator is treated as a file the operator chose & is
  verified where they put it. A Binary with no separator is a name & is looked up.
  FIX. Correct the path in the half of the manifest that holds the entry, Resources/Engine/depends.php
  or Resources/Contract/depends.php, or make the file executable if it is there & is not.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DEPENDENCY STATUS
  refused

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/dependencyCore.php

DESCRIPTION
  A policy gate refused this entry before it was probed. The tool may well be installed &
  working. Nothing was checked, because the entry was not allowed to be checked.
  Two settings can cause it & the reported reason names which one.
  --Engine Enforce GPL Compliant Deps-- refuses an entry declaring no license, or one not
  on the compatible list. GPL-2.0 only is deliberately absent from that list, because it is
  not compatible with GPLv3.
  --Engine Allow Incomplete Deps-- refuses an entry missing License, Source or Purpose.
  FIX. Complete the entry in the half of the manifest that holds it, Resources/Engine/depends.php or
  Resources/Contract/depends.php, or turn the setting off in engineConfig.php if the policy is not
  one this installation wants.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-33000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A dependency operation was attempted without a valid authorization token!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/dependencyCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An install, update or uninstall was called without a token derived from the install secret.
  The token proves the request arrived through convertCore.php rather than by another route.
  The install secret was rotated between the token being derived & being validated.
  The install secret could not be read, so no valid token could be derived.
  The component was reached some way that does not go through the --setup argument.
  Reading the machine needs no token at all. --check-depends & --output-supply-chain are
  unaffected by this & remain available.
  This error is not fatal. Nothing was installed, updated or removed.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-33001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A supply chain report could not be written!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/dependencyCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The comma separated report could not be written to the destination.
  The report is always written beside the logs. A path supplied with --output-supply-chain only
  names the file, & the folder it names is ignored.
  The log directory is not writable by this account.
  As root, the log directory belongs to the web server account, so root writes the report into
  the data location instead, & a WARNING names it. This error means that fallback failed too,
  because the data location is missing or another account could replace it.
  The filesystem is full.
  This error is not fatal. Nothing else is affected & the report may be requested again with a
  destination this account owns.

  performs it could not be loaded.
  Dependency Core is absent, reports no version, or reports a version this core does not require.
  Run -v & read the Dependency Core line, which names the version found & the version wanted.

  The guard is written INTO the generated file, so it appears in source as a string inside a string.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! HRConvert2-34000, A pipeline configuration cannot be loaded directly!
  ERROR!!! HRConvert2-34000, A pipeline converter cannot be loaded directly!
  ERROR!!! HRConvert2-34000, A pipeline component cannot be loaded directly!

FILE
  /Resources/Pipelines/<PIPELINE_NAME>/pipelineConfig.php
  /Resources/Pipelines/<PIPELINE_NAME>/pipeline.php
  /Resources/Pipelines/Shared/<MODULE_NAME>.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  NO

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A file belonging to the pipeline component system was executed directly instead of being loaded by convertCore.php.
  Every file in that system opens by testing whether the core has already been loaded, and refuses to run when it has not.
  A component reached directly has no core beneath it, so quickDie() does not exist and cannot be used to halt.
  This is the one documented case in HRConvert2 where a plain die() is the correct way to stop.
  Because this error takes place before any logging environment belonging to the request exists, this error is not logged.
  This is most commonly seen when somebody requests a file under /Resources/Pipelines/ directly in a browser.
  Confirm that every folder beneath /Resources/Pipelines/ contains the stock index.html file.
  A folder without one is a readable directory listing on a path a user can reach, which is the usual reason this is ever seen.
  Do not write a new index.html file. Copy an existing one from elsewhere in the installation.
  Until v3.9.9 the Pipeline Core raised this too, as The Pipeline Core component cannot be loaded directly.
  It is an Engine core now & raises Engine-35000 like the others.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The Pipeline Core could not verify a single conversion pipeline!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/pipelineCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The Pipeline Core loaded and passed its own version check, but not one of the pipelines it accepts could be verified.
  A dispatcher with nothing behind it is worse than no dispatcher at all, so the Pipeline Core stands itself down when this happens.
  An application that accepts no pipeline at all converts nothing, & from v4.3.2 that raises nothing & -v says NONE. Until then it raised this too.
  No conversion of any kind will succeed for the remainder of the request.
  Uploading, downloading, deleting, archiving and virus scanning all continue to work normally.
  This error is not fatal because a user who cannot read the page cannot report the problem.
  Every pipeline that was refused produced its own warning immediately above this line, naming the folder and the reason it was refused.
  Run the application with the -v argument and read those warnings. A version mismatch names both the version found and the version required.
  This is most commonly seen when the /Resources/Pipelines/ directory is missing, unreadable by the web server user, or was not deployed.
  It is also seen when convertCore.php and the pipeline folders come from different releases, because every pipeline is an EXACT version match.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The <PIPELINE_NAME> pipeline declared the entry point <FUNCTION_NAME>, which does not exist! Its converter is expected at <FILE_PATH>.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/pipelineCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A pipeline named a function in its pipelineConfig.php file that does not exist once its pipelineCore.php file has been loaded.
  The pipeline cannot be dispatched to. Any lower priority implementation of the same conversion family is tried before the request gives up.
  This is most commonly caused by copying an existing pipeline folder to create a new one and renaming the entry point in only one of the two files.
  The value of $PipelineEntryPoint in pipelineConfig.php must match the function actually defined in pipelineCore.php exactly, including case.
  This is also seen when pipelineCore.php is missing from a folder whose configuration expects one.
  It is also seen when a shared module the pipeline declared failed to load, taking the functions the converter depends on with it.
  Check the warnings immediately above this line for a refused shared module before looking anywhere else.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34003, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The <FILE_TYPE> pipeline failed to convert <FILE_TYPE> to <FILE_TYPE> & reported no reason!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34003, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: All <ADDITIONAL_DATA> installed <FILE_TYPE> pipelines failed to convert <FILE_TYPE> to <FILE_TYPE> & none reported a reason!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/pipelineCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A pipeline failed to produce the requested output and gave no account of why.
  THIS ERROR DOES NOT FIRE WHEN A PIPELINE EXPLAINS ITSELF.
  A pipeline that fails and logs its own error under its own number has already been heard, and restating that here would misreport the origin of the failure and double the volume of the log.
  A REFUSAL IS NOT A FAILURE. Stream inspection denying a playlist is HRConvert2 working exactly as designed, and the Stream pipeline reports that under its own number with the actual reason attached.
  This error therefore indicates a genuine defect in the pipeline named in the message rather than a problem with the file being converted.
  The usual cause is a converter that returned early on a code path carrying no errorEntry() or warningEntry() call.
  Where two or more implementations of one conversion family are installed, each is tried in priority order and this fires only once the last of them has failed. The message then names how many were tried.
  Read the warnings immediately above this line first. If there are none at all, the fault is on whichever path the named pipeline took.
  The request continues after this error and reports the conversion as failed.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34004, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The <PIPELINE_NAME> pipeline declared the entry point <FUNCTION_NAME>, which the <PIPELINE_NAME> pipeline already claimed. It was refused.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/pipelineCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Two installed pipelines declared the same entry point function name. The first pipeline to claim the name keeps it and the second is refused.
  This is the single most likely mistake made when writing a pipeline by copying an existing one, because the entry point must be renamed in both pipelineConfig.php and pipeline.php.
  The refusal is deliberate and is preferable to the alternative.
  Two pipelines sharing a function name would fatally redeclare that function the moment both were loaded in one request, which happens whenever a single upload batch spans two conversion families, and again whenever a fallback candidate loads after a primary implementation failed.
  Give the new pipeline a unique entry point name in both of its files, then raise the pipeline's version & its pin in $EngineAcceptedPipelines, which Resources/Contract/app-engine-config.php sets.
  The message names both folders, so the one to correct is the one reported as refused.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The <FILE_TYPE> conversion family has no installed pipeline!

FILE
  /convertCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A conversion was requested for a family that no installed pipeline serves.
  The family's pipeline folder is missing, unreadable, or reports a version the Pipeline Core does not accept, or the family is not named in $EngineAcceptedPipelines in Resources/Contract/app-engine-config.php.
  Run the application with the -v argument and read the warnings above this line. They name the folder and the reason it was refused.
  RESTORING A CONVERTER TO convertCore.php IS NOT THE FIX FOR THIS ERROR.
  convertCore.php carried a built in dispatcher throughout the migration to pipeline components, and each conversion family left that dispatcher individually as its code moved out into its own folder.
  It became empty once the last family migrated and it has been removed.
  Pasting a converter back into the core would produce a second implementation that the Pipeline Core knows nothing about, cannot version check, and cannot rank against the real one.
  Repair the pipeline folder instead.
  From v4.3.2 a model in x, 3mf or dxf ends here where config.php still offers it as a model input, because the Model pipeline
  reads none of them & no longer declares them. Error 9005 says why, & the three should be taken out of
  --Supported Model Input Formats--.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The <OPERATION_FAMILY> operation was requested but no pipeline provides it!
  Until v3.9.9 the Pipeline Core worded this OCR was requested but no OCR pipeline is installed!, because it
  could only dispatch OCR. It names whatever operation family was requested now. Until v4.1.6 it began An, as
  An OCR operation, which read wrongly for every family but OCR.
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: OCR was requested but no OCR pipeline is installed & this core has no built in OCR!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Identify was requested but no Identify pipeline is installed!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Extract was requested but no Extractor pipeline is installed!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Archive was requested but no Archiver pipeline is installed!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A virus scan was requested but no Scan pipeline is installed!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Strip Metadata was requested but no Metadata pipeline is installed!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Merge was requested but no PDF pipeline is installed!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Split was requested but no PDF pipeline is installed!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Extract was requested but no Extractor pipeline is installed!

FILE
  /convertCore.php
  /Resources/Engine/Cores/pipelineCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An Optical Character Recognition operation was requested and the OCR pipeline could not be reached.
  The /Resources/Pipelines/OCR/ folder is missing, unreadable, or reports a version the Pipeline Core does not accept, or 'OCR' is not named in $EngineAcceptedPipelines in Resources/Contract/app-engine-config.php.
  It is also raised when the Pipeline Core itself failed to verify, because there is no built in OCR left in convertCore.php to fall back to.
  Run the application with the -v argument and read the warnings above this line.
  This error is not itself fatal, but error 22 fires immediately after it and that error is fatal.
  Without this error, an OCR request on an installation with no OCR pipeline would produce an empty result and raise only error 22, which reports that the OCR operation failed and does not report that no OCR was ever attempted.
  OCR is an operation pipeline rather than a conversion pipeline. It accepts a selection of files, chooses a different route for each one depending on whether it is a PDF, a document or an image, and returns a single verdict for the whole batch along with the list of files it produced.
  Since v4.1.2 Identify is an operation pipeline too, & the same reasoning applies to it. Its folder is /Resources/Pipelines/Identify/ & ERROR 31 follows this one in its place.
  Since v4.1.6 archiving a selection of files & scanning files for a user are operation pipelines as well, the Archiver pipeline in
  /Resources/Pipelines/Archiver/ & the Scan pipeline in /Resources/Pipelines/Scan/. ERROR 20 follows the Archiver's & ERROR 23 the Scan's.
  The core says which, by the second & third wordings above, when the Pipeline Core itself is unavailable, & the Pipeline Core says
  which by the first when it is there & no pipeline of that family is installed.
  Where the pipeline is missing, a page offers no button for it & a workflow offers no step for it, so a page this server drew does
  not ask for it. A request made by hand, or a page drawn before the pipeline was removed, still can.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34007, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A virus scan was requested & no scanner was named or configured!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/pipelineCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Nothing asked for a particular scanner & --Default Virus Scanner-- in config.php is
  empty, so there was nothing to run.
  Set it to scancore, which ships inside this application, or to clamav if ClamAV is
  installed on this host.
  THE FILE WAS NOT SCANNED. Whether that stops the operation is the caller's decision &
  is reported separately.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34008, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The <SCANNER> scanner was requested & no pipeline provides it!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/pipelineCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A scanner was named & no installed pipeline declares that family.
  A scanner is chosen by NAME rather than by file type, so the name in config.php must
  match a scanner pipeline's family exactly, allowing for case.
  Run -v to list what is installed. Two ship with this application, ScanCore & ClamAV.
  This is deliberately NOT a fall back to whatever scanner happens to be present. A scan
  that quietly used a different scanner than the one an administrator configured is worse
  than a scan that refused, because the log would say a scan ran.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34009, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The file is already <FILE_TYPE>, so there is nothing to convert!

FILE
  /convertCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A conversion asked for the format the file already has, & no installed pipeline converts a format to itself.
  Until v4.0.6 this was reported as 34005, a family with no installed pipeline, which sent an operator looking for a fault that was not there.
  From v4.2.8 such a request is never given to a family other than the one that claims it. The Ebook pipeline excludes txt, rtf,
  docx & pdf, the Archive pipeline rar & tgz, & the Stream pipeline ts & m3u8, each to itself, as every other pipeline excludes its own.
