----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-34010, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The <PIPELINE> pipeline's result was refused, & no other pipeline claims the conversion. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/pipelineCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A pipeline said it had converted a file, & what it left is not a result. What follows the message is one of these.
    The converter left <FILE_NAME> empty, so the conversion failed & the empty file was removed!
    The converter left <FILE_NAME> as something other than a file, so the conversion failed!
    The converter reported success & left nothing at <FILE_NAME>, so the conversion failed!
  Until v4.3.1 the sentence alone was the message. The message names the pipeline whose result was refused from then.
  A converter that fails part way can leave an empty file, as ImageMagick does for an icon it cannot write, & Calibre writes a folder for OEB.
  An empty file is removed. A folder or a link is left for the sweep, which removes the session it is in.
  A pipeline that reports success & leaves nothing at all is named too, so the failure is never silent.
  Where another installed pipeline could still convert the file, this is a warning instead, & that pipeline is tried next.
  The warning names the pipeline as well, & is in WARNINGS ABOUT PIPELINES.
  A plain text format may be empty, because a document or an image with no text in it converts to an empty one, so txt, csv, tsv, md & the subtitle formats are not held to this.
  Until v4.0.6 every pipeline judged by whether anything was there, so each of these was offered to the user as the result.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR NUMBER RESERVATION
  34011 through 34019 are reserved for the Pipeline Core and are currently unused.
  34009 is the core's, as 34005 is, & 34010 is the Pipeline Core's. Both are documented above.
  34007 & 34008 are its scanner errors & are documented above, with the rest of the block.
  A conversion pipeline carries its own error block and keeps the numbers it had before it became a component.
  A new community pipeline must reserve a block in this file before using one.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WARNINGS ABOUT PIPELINES
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  The Pipeline Core raises these, from Resources/Engine/Cores/pipelineCore.php. Until v4.2.1 none of them was documented here.
    WARNING!!! The application's <LIST> is not a list, so nothing in it was accepted.
    WARNING!!! The application's <LIST> names <NAME>, which is not a plain name. It was refused.
    WARNING!!! The application's <LIST> pins <NAME> to something that is not a version. It was refused.
      <LIST> is the list of accepted pipelines or of accepted shared modules, which Resources/Contract/app-engine-config.php sets.
      A bad entry costs that entry & nothing else.
    WARNING!!! A pipeline asked for the shared module <NAME>, which this Pipeline Core does not accept.
    WARNING!!! The shared module <NAME> is not installed at <FILE_PATH>.
    WARNING!!! The shared module <NAME> could not be read.
    WARNING!!! The shared module <NAME> reports no version & was refused.
    WARNING!!! The shared module <NAME> reports v<VERSION> & this manager requires v<VERSION>. It was refused.
      A shared module the pipeline declared did not load, so the functions its converter calls may not exist. 34002 usually follows.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline is not installed at <FOLDER>.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline has no pipelineConfig.php at <FILE_PATH>.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline configuration at <FILE_PATH> could not be read.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline reports no version & was refused.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline reports v<VERSION> & this manager requires v<VERSION>. It was refused.
      An accepted pipeline is missing, cannot be read or is not the version it is pinned to, which must match exactly. It is not offered.
      Run -v, which counts the pipelines of each kind that verified.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline declared no family & was refused.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline declared an unusable entry point name & was refused.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline declared no usable capabilities & was refused.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline declared capabilities that are not arrays & was refused.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline declared an empty capability list & was refused.
      Its pipelineConfig.php leaves out or misstates something every pipeline declares. ABOUT_PIPELINE_COMPONENTS lists each.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline declared a usage naming nothing recognized. It was left at web & gui.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline declares $PipelineRequiredDependencies as something other than an array, so it requires nothing.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline declares a required dependency that names no tool. It was left out.
      From v4.2.1. $PipelineRequiredDependencies names the tools a pipeline cannot run without, by the names the dependency manifest
      gives them, & a pipeline is offered only where they are installed. A requirement that cannot be read requires nothing, so a
      mistake in declaring one never hides a pipeline that works.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline cannot run without <NAME>, which the dependency manifest does not name, so it is believed installed.
      From v4.2.1. A required tool is looked for by its manifest entry, & a name the manifest lacks cannot be looked for, so the
      pipeline is offered. The name must match the manifest's exactly. --pipeline --check-depends names every one that does not.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline's result was refused, so the next pipeline that claims the conversion is tried. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
      From v4.3.1. A pipeline said it converted a file & what it left is not a result, as 34010 describes, & another installed pipeline
      claims the conversion, so it is tried next. What follows says what was left. 34010 is raised once no pipeline is left to try.
    WARNING!!! Capability mismatch. The <FAMILY> pipeline claims <FILE_TYPE> to <FILE_TYPE> & <SUBSYSTEM> reports it cannot. A narrowing mode would refuse this conversion. Check the alias table in depends.php before enabling one.
      Raised only in the detected-advisory mode, which converts as declared & says what a narrowing mode would have refused.
      ABOUT_CAPABILITY_DETECTION describes the modes.
    WARNING!!! Dispatch asked for the <PIPELINE> pipeline, which was never verified.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline permits <USAGE> & was asked for from <SURFACE>. It was refused.
      A caller asked for a pipeline that did not verify, or from a surface its $PipelineUsage does not permit. Nothing ran.
    WARNING!!! The dependency manifest could not be read, so <NAME> could not be verified.
    WARNING!!! The Dependency Core is unavailable, so <NAME> could not be verified.
    WARNING!!! No manifest entry is named <NAME>, so it could not be verified.
    WARNING!!! <NAME> is <STATUS> & will not be used.
      A pipeline asked for a tool before running it, & it was not verified. The pipeline's own error follows.
      <STATUS> is what --setup --check-depends says of the tool. A tool the manifest does not mark Required is logged rather than warned about.
    WARNING!!! The <FAMILY> pipeline dispatcher was called for <FILE_TYPE> to <FILE_TYPE>, which no installed pipeline claims.
    WARNING!!! The <PIPELINE> pipeline could not complete a <FILE_TYPE> to <FILE_TYPE> conversion. Falling back to the next implementation.
      Where more than one installed pipeline converts a pair, the next is tried when one fails, & each is named as it fails.

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  NO

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php was requested directly, rather than loaded by an application's entry point.
  The kernel cannot test $CoreLoaded like every other component, because it loads before anything sets it.
  It counts the files PHP has included instead. A direct request is the only way it can be the first one.
  Nothing is wrong. Something asked for a file it should not have.

ERROR MESSAGE

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Identify/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Every tool that reads a file for Identify may write only into a folder made for the request inside the session, which is removed when the request ends.
  That folder could not be made, so no tool was run & no file was read.
  The session folder is missing, or the web server account cannot write to it.
  Sessions are kept in Sessions in the data location, which only the web server account may write. -fp gives each managed location its owner & mode. See Documentation/ABOUT_MANAGED_LOCATIONS.txt.
  The device where data is stored has no space left.
  This error is not fatal on its own. No file was identified, so ERROR 31 follows & that error is fatal.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-37001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: No free name was found for the Identify report, so it was not written!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Identify/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A report was asked for under a name the session already holds, & so was every numbered name after it.
  A report is never written over a file a user already has. A taken name is given _1, then _2, as every conversion output is, up to _1000.
  A session holding a thousand copies of one name is not normal use. The session is being filled on purpose, or a script is asking for reports in a loop.
  Ask for the report under another name, or delete the old reports.
  This error is not fatal on its own. The files were identified & the reply still describes them, but ERROR 31 follows because the report asked for was not written.

  NO

LOGGED
  YES

  The device where data is stored has no space left, or no inodes left.
  This error is not fatal on its own. The files were identified & the reply still describes them, but ERROR 31 follows because the report asked for was not written.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-37004, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so no tool could read the files being identified!
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-37004, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so not every tool could read the files being identified!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Identify/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  Every tool Identify runs on a file runs inside the sandbox, & the sandbox could not be built.
  The first form says config.php requires a sandbox, so no tool was run.
  The setting is --Require Sandbox--, or --Require Sandbox On Docker-- in a container.
  Each file was still read by PHP, so its size, its date & its hashes are reported, & a note on each says no tool read it.
  The type, the character set & every detail a tool reads are missing from what is reported.
  The second form is from v4.2.7. config.php does not require a sandbox, so the tools ran unprotected, except FFprobe, which runs
  under the ffmpeg sandbox profile. That profile sets RequireSandbox & is never run without one, so the details of an audio or a
  video file were not read, & a note on each such file says so. Neither setting changes that. From v4.3.1 ImageMagick, which
  reads an image's details under the imagemagick profile, is never run without one either.
  This is logged once for the whole selection rather than once for every tool & every file.
  A WARNING!!! above it, from the sandbox, says why the sandbox could not be built.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  as root & read the Sandbox line. Bubblewrap is a required dependency.
  Turning either setting off would let every other tool read the files unprotected. Repair the sandbox instead.
  This error is not fatal. Every file that was hashed is still in the reply.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-38000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The application names no operation provider, so no workflow can run. It is named by $EngineOperationProvider in <INSTALLATION>/Resources/Contract/app-engine-config.php.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/workflowCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The Workflow Core asks the application which operations a workflow may run, through the function $EngineOperationProvider names.
  That name is empty, or names a function the application does not define, so there are no operations & no workflow runs.
  HRConvert2 names applicationOperations(). A contract file older than v4.1.4, or one edited without that line, causes this.
  Every page that asks for workflows is told they are unavailable, & --workflow --list & --workflow --check say no operation is offered.
  Nothing else changes. Every button still works.
  Unpack the release again & run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp , which installs the contract it ships.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-38001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The operation provider <FUNCTION_NAME> returned no list of operations, so no workflow can run.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/workflowCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The function the contract names was called, & what it returned is not an array holding Operations as an array.
  An application's provider returns array('Operations' => array(...), 'FileTypes' => array(...)). See ABOUT_WORKFLOWS.txt.
  In HRConvert2 that function is applicationOperations() in convertCore.php, & a core & a contract from different releases is the likely cause.
  Every page that asks for workflows is told they are unavailable, & every button still works.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  & check the core & the Engine report the same release.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-38002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: A workflow could not be started. <REASON>

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/workflowCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The session was claimed & the worker that runs the workflow did not start. The claim is given back, so the user may try again.
  The page is told the workflow could not be started. <REASON> says which of these it was.
    Its job could not be written.                       The session's Workflow folder could not be written. Check the data location is not full & is the web server account's.
    No install secret is available ...                  The worker is given a startup key derived from the install secret, & none could be read. Run -fp as root.
    setsid or php could not be found ...                Both are needed to start a worker. php is, by definition, & setsid is part of util-linux.
    PHP has proc_open disabled ...                      disable_functions in the web server's PHP configuration names proc_open. Workflows need it.
    The worker process could not be created.            The operating system refused a new process. Read the web server's error log.
    setsid exited with status <N> ...                   setsid could not hand the worker its own session.
  A worker that starts & then cannot take the job up is a different fault. Its status says interrupted, with the reason start,
  & a warning in the log beginning A workflow worker was refused says why.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-38003, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The <OPERATION> operation failed with <EXCEPTION_CLASS>. <EXCEPTION_MESSAGE>

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/workflowCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  An operation a workflow step ran threw an exception or an error, rather than returning that it failed.
  The Workflow Core catches it, treats every file in that call as failed & goes on, so one failure does not halt the worker.
  A step whose every file failed ends the workflow, & the page is told a step of the workflow failed.
  <EXCEPTION_CLASS> & <EXCEPTION_MESSAGE> are PHP's own. An operation should report a failure by returning it, so this is a fault in the
  operation's code or in a tool it calls. The entries before it in the Workflow log name the files it was given.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-38004, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Workflow job <JOB_ID> halted before it ended. The entry before this one says why.

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/workflowCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A workflow worker stopped while its workflow was still running. A fatal PHP error halts a process this way, & so does a fatal error the
  application raises, such as a virus found by the scan --Virus Scanning-- runs on every conversion.
  The worker's shutdown handler writes the status as failed, with the reason halted, & gives back the session & the server's count of
  running workflows, so the page is told the workflow stopped unexpectedly rather than waiting for it.
  The entry before this one, in the Workflow log, or PHP's own error in the web server's error log, says what halted the worker.
  Every file the workflow made before it halted is still in the session.
  This error is not fatal on its own. It is the report of a halt that already happened.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-38005, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The workflow <WORKFLOW_ID> could not be installed. <REASON>

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/workflowCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  --workflow --install checked the workflow, found it valid & could not write it into the installed library. From v4.2.2
  --install-staged raises it as well, & so does a workflow that was written & whose descriptions were not, where <REASON> begins
  The workflow was installed & its descriptions were not. Install it again to install them.
  The installed library is the Workflows folder in the data root. <REASON> says which of these it was.
    The installed library does not exist yet.           -fp creates it. Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp  & install again.
    A reason naming a folder another account can change  Root writes only into a folder no other account can change. -fp gives the
                                                          library & the folders above it back to root.
    A reason from the write                              The kernel refused to write through a link, or the disk is full or read only.
  Nothing was installed, & the library is as it was. The command exits 1.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-38006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The workflow <WORKFLOW_ID> could not be removed. <REASON>

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/workflowCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  --workflow --remove found the installed workflow & could not remove it.
  Root removes a file only from a folder no other account can change, & never through a link. <REASON> is the kernel's.
  Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp , which gives the library back to root, & remove it again.
  A shipped workflow is part of the release & is never removed by this command. To replace one, install a workflow with the same identifier.
  From v4.2.2 its descriptions are removed with it. Where the workflow was removed & its descriptions were not, <REASON> begins
  The workflow <WORKFLOW_ID> was removed & its descriptions were not. Remove the file named for it followed by -descriptions.json
  from the installed library by hand.
  The command exits 1.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-38008, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The staged workflow <WORKFLOW_ID> could not be discarded. <REASON>

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/workflowCore.php

  YES

  From v4.2.2. --workflow --discard-staged found a workflow staged under <WORKFLOW_ID> & could not remove it, or its descriptions,
  from Staged. Root hands the discard to the web server account, which owns Staged, so this is that account's error, & root prints
  what it printed. <REASON> is the kernel's. The descriptions are removed first, so a workflow is never left staged without them.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR NUMBER RESERVATION
  38000 through 38019 are reserved for workflows, & 38009 through 38019 are currently unused.
  38000 through 38008 are raised by the Workflow Core, & are documented above.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WARNINGS ABOUT WORKFLOWS
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  The Workflow Core raises these, from Resources/Engine/Cores/workflowCore.php.
    WARNING!!! The operation provider declared an operation whose name is not a plain word. It was left out.
    WARNING!!! The <OPERATION> operation names no function that exists. It was left out.
    WARNING!!! The <OPERATION> operation names no budget. One that takes none says so with a Budget of FALSE. It was left out.
    WARNING!!! The <OPERATION> operation declares an option that is not usable. It was left out of the operation.
    WARNING!!! The <OPERATION> operation declares no mode it runs in. It was left out.
    WARNING!!! The <OPERATION> operation names <FUNCTION_NAME> to decide which files it reads, & no such function exists. It was left out.
    WARNING!!! The <OPERATION> operation names <FUNCTION_NAME> to decide which files it passes on unchanged, & no such function exists. It was left out.
      An entry the operation provider declared cannot be used. That operation is left out, & every other one is offered.
      From v4.1.8 an operation that takes no budget declares a Budget of FALSE, as HRConvert2's Delete & Rename do. A budget that
      is missing or empty is still a mistake, & leaves that operation out.
    WARNING!!! The <OPERATION> operation says it consumes its files in a way this core does not know. It is read as leaving them as they were.
      From v4.1.8. Consumes is deleted, renamed or empty. Any other value is read as empty, so the operation is offered & a step
      taking the files it took is not refused, which is the safer mistake. The operation provider should be corrected.
    WARNING!!! The <OPERATION> operation's <OPTION> option declares Offered as something other than a list. Every one of its choices is offered.
      From v4.2.1. A choice option may name the choices this server runs in Offered, & its Choices are then every choice the
      application knows. One whose Offered cannot be read offers every choice, so a mistake never hides one that works.
    WARNING!!! The operation provider <FUNCTION_NAME> declared Select, which is the Workflow Core's own step. It was left out.
      From v4.1.5. The name Select is the Workflow Core's, so an application's operation of that name is never run.
    WARNING!!! The operation provider <FUNCTION_NAME> names <FUNCTION_NAME> to list a session's files, & no such function exists. Select is not offered.
      From v4.1.5. Every other operation is offered, & a workflow with a Select step does not check until the function exists.
    WARNING!!! The operation provider <FUNCTION_NAME> returned no usable operation, so no workflow can run.
    WARNING!!! The <LIBRARY> workflow <FILE_NAME> is not valid & is not offered. Run --workflow --list to read why.
      From v4.2.1 a workflow naming a format or a scanner this server does not offer is valid & is not warned about. It is listed as
      disabled, as one using an operation that is turned off is, & is offered wherever the tool it needs is installed.
      From v4.2.2 <LIBRARY> may be staged, for a workflow --workflow --list reads in Staged, which is never offered until root
      installs it, & --install-staged refuses one that is not valid.
    WARNING!!! The descriptions of the <LIBRARY> workflow <WORKFLOW_ID> are not valid & are not shown. Run --workflow --list to read why.
      From v4.2.2. A workflow's descriptions are kept beside it, named for its identifier followed by -descriptions.json. The
      workflow is offered with its own Description, & --workflow --list names every problem with them. Correct the file, or stage
      the workflow again with its descriptions & install it.
    WARNING!!! A request asked to stage a workflow for every user, & is not signed in as an administrator where staging is offered. It was refused.
      From v4.2.2. Only a page signed in to an account whose role is admin is offered staging, where the builder & import & export
      are on. A request that asks otherwise was made by hand, or its sign in ended, or its role changed, since its page was drawn.
      Nothing was staged.
    WARNING!!! The staged workflow <WORKFLOW_ID> is installed & could not be cleared from the staging folder. <REASON>
      From v4.2.2. Root installed the workflow, & the web server account could not remove the staged copy. It is harmless, because
      installing it again changes nothing. <REASON> is the kernel's. -fp gives Staged back to the web server account.
    WARNING!!! A session's workflow folder could not be made ready, so no workflow can run in it.
    WARNING!!! The folder running workflows are counted in could not be locked, so the limit on running workflows is not applied to this one.
    WARNING!!! A workflow was asked to stop & the request could not be recorded.
    WARNING!!! The <OPERATION> operation reported a file that is not in the session, so the workflow does not pass it on.
    WARNING!!! Detached worker <PROCESS_IDENTIFIER> was killed before it finished, so the workflow goes on without its output.
    WARNING!!! A workflow worker was refused. <REASON>
    WARNING!!! Workflow job <JOB_ID> did not run step <STEP_NUMBER>, <OPERATION>, because an earlier step had errors. The files it would have deleted are kept.
      From v4.1.8. A step that deletes files does not run once an earlier step of the same workflow has had errors, because the
      files it would delete may be the only copies of what that step failed to make. The workflow ends as failed, with the reason
      incomplete, & nothing is deleted. The entries before this one name each error. Fix what failed & run the workflow again.
    WARNING!!! The Workflow Core is unavailable. No workflow can run until it is repaired.
    WARNING!!! A workflow worker was started without a session it can continue.
  The core raises these, from convertCore.php.
    WARNING!!! A workflow was requested & the Workflow Core is unavailable.
    WARNING!!! A workflow was requested & workflows are disabled.
    WARNING!!! A workflow's virus scan report could not be kept beside the files it scanned.
    WARNING!!! A request carried <INPUT> as something other than text, & it was refused.
      From v4.1.5. streamBaseURL, userURLDownload, workflowDocument & workflowCheck are read as text, & so is
      workflowPreviewDocument from v4.1.8, & workflowStage & workflowStageDescriptions from v4.2.2. A list sent in place of one
      is read as nothing. No page this server draws sends one, so a request that does was made by hand.
  The Engine raises these, from Resources/Engine/engine.php.
    WARNING!!! The command <COMMAND> was refused because the Workflow Core is unavailable.
    WARNING!!! A workflow command that writes the installed library was refused for an unauthorized user.
      --workflow --install, --install-staged & --remove write the installed library, which is root's, so only root runs them.
      The refusal repeats the option as it was typed, from v4.2.2, & shows the command to run with sudo. Nothing was changed.
    WARNING!!! A workflow command that discards a staged workflow was refused for an unauthorized user.
      From v4.2.2. --workflow --discard-staged removes names in Staged, which is the web server account's, so only root, which
      hands it to that account, & that account run it. The refusal shows the command to run with sudo. Nothing was changed.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-39000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed 7-Zip version is missing, unidentifiable, or too old, so <ARCHIVE_NAME> was not extracted!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Shared/archiveExtraction.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  No 7-Zip this application accepts was found, so the archive was not opened at all.
  This application builds its own 7-Zip where the packaged one is older than depends.php accepts.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  & read the 7-Zip line. Run  sudo php convertCore.php --setup --install-depends  to install it.
  The Extractor & the Archive pipeline both raise this, through the archiveExtraction.php shared module.

FILE

  NO

  YES

  YES

  The web server account needs read & write access to the session folder & everything below it.
  The device where data is stored has an insufficient amount of storage space available.
  A folder that is not empty is refused rather than written into, because what is in it is not the archive's.
  Whatever was written is removed with the folder.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WARNINGS ABOUT EXTRACTING ARCHIVES
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  An archive is a file a stranger wrote, so each refusal of one is a warning. Nothing was wrong with this server.
  The archiveExtraction.php shared module raises these for the Extractor & the Archive pipeline, from Resources/Pipelines/Shared/archiveExtraction.php.
    WARNING!!! --Maximum Extracted Size-- in config.php is missing or is not a whole number of 0 or more, so an extraction is held to <MEGABYTES> megabytes.
    WARNING!!! --Maximum Extracted Files-- in config.php is missing or is not a whole number of 0 or more, so an extraction is held to <COUNT> files & folders.
      A missing or unreadable limit is the default, & never no limit. Run  sudo php convertCore.php --config --repair .
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> is protected by a password, so nothing was extracted from it.
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> holds no files, so nothing was extracted from it.
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> holds <COUNT> files & folders, more than the <LIMIT> --Maximum Extracted Files-- allows, so nothing was extracted from it.
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> holds <SIZE>, more than the <SIZE> --Maximum Extracted Size-- allows, so nothing was extracted from it.
      An archive is listed before anything is written, & one that says it holds too much is refused on its own word.
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> was not extracted, because the disk holding this session cannot take it. <REASON>
      The Resource Manager refused the space the archive said it needs. This only occurs with resource awareness active.
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> grew past the <SIZE> --Maximum Extracted Size-- allows as it was extracted, so it was stopped & nothing it wrote was kept.
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> made more than the <LIMIT> files & folders --Maximum Extracted Files-- allows as it was extracted, so it was stopped & nothing it wrote was kept.
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> was still being extracted after an hour, so it was stopped & nothing it wrote was kept.
      An archive that lies about what it holds is measured while 7-Zip writes it & is stopped at the limit, which is what these say.
    WARNING!!! 7-Zip reported a problem with the archive <ARCHIVE_NAME>, such as a damaged file or a link leading out of it, so nothing it wrote was kept.
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> lists more than this server reads, so nothing was extracted from it.
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> could not be listed within five minutes, so nothing was extracted from it.
    WARNING!!! 7-Zip could not read <ARCHIVE_NAME> as an archive, so nothing was extracted from it.
    WARNING!!! What 7-Zip extracted from <ARCHIVE_NAME> could not all be read back, so none of it was kept.
    WARNING!!! The archive <ARCHIVE_NAME> held nothing 7-Zip could extract, so nothing was extracted from it.
  The Extractor pipeline raises these, from Resources/Pipelines/Extractor/pipeline.php.
    WARNING!!! The file <FILE_NAME> is not an archive this server extracts, so nothing was extracted from it.
    WARNING!!! A virus was found in <FILE_NAME>, so nothing was extracted from it.
    WARNING!!! A virus was found in what <FILE_NAME> held, so nothing was extracted from it.
      An infected archive is refused & left where it is. What happens to it after is --User Virus Scanning--'s, which a pipeline cannot read.
    WARNING!!! Left out <COUNT> link(s) or special file(s) <FILE_NAME> held, because only files are extracted.
    WARNING!!! Left out <COUNT> file(s) <FILE_NAME> held, whose names or formats this server does not accept.
      A file is placed only under a name & a format an upload could have, so a name such as index.html or a .php file is left out.
    WARNING!!! What was extracted from <FILE_NAME> could not all be read back, so none of it was kept.
    WARNING!!! The archive <FILE_NAME> held no files, so nothing was extracted from it.
    WARNING!!! The folder <FILE_NAME> was extracted into could not be removed. It is removed with the session.
  The Archive pipeline raises this, from Resources/Pipelines/Archive/pipeline.php.
    WARNING!!! The folder <FILE_NAME> was extracted into could not be removed. It is removed with the session.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WARNINGS ABOUT FETCHING A URL FOR A USER
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  A user fetch is refused for an address a stranger chose, so each refusal is a warning. Nothing was wrong with this server.
  The core raises these, from convertCore.php.
    WARNING!!! A URL download was requested & the feature is disabled.
    WARNING!!! A session reached its URL download limit of <COUNT>.
    WARNING!!! A user requested download did not complete. <REASON>
      <REASON> is what the user is shown. The address was refused because it resolves to a private, reserved or loopback
      address, its name could not be resolved or it did not pass inspection. The address was accepted & nothing could be
      fetched from it, which includes a server that answered with an error page, a redirect, or more than the ceiling.
      The fetched file was refused by a scanner, could not be scanned, names no file type, is a type this server does not
      handle, or could not be placed in the session. Or the address was not one, & says why in the words of a base address.
  The Engine raises these, from Resources/Engine/engine.php.
    WARNING!!! --URL Download Maximum Bytes-- is not a whole number of 0 or more, so the default of <BYTES> bytes is used.
      From v4.1.7. 0 is no ceiling, as config.php says, & anything that is not a whole number is two gigabytes, & never no ceiling.
    WARNING!!! A fetched file was refused. <FINDING>
    WARNING!!! A private address was reached because <RANGE> is a permitted range.
      --Permitted Private Ranges-- allowed it. Each is written, so an allowance an administrator forgot is seen in the log.
    WARNING!!! An address in <RANGE> was refused. That range is never permitted, whatever is configured.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WARNINGS ABOUT INSTALLING DEPENDENCIES
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  Dependency Core raises these while --setup --install-depends runs, from Resources/Engine/Cores/dependencyCore.php.
    WARNING!!! The package index could not be refreshed. Installs may fail or fetch stale versions.
    WARNING!!! Refusing to install <NAME>. <REASON>
    WARNING!!! Skipping <NAME>. <REASON> Nothing was changed.
      The manifest entry is not one this release may act on. <REASON> says what it lacks.
    WARNING!!! The source build for <NAME> exited with code <CODE>.
    WARNING!!! Dependency <NAME> could not be installed. <OUTPUT>
      <OUTPUT> is the end of what the install or the build printed. The whole of it is in the log.
    WARNING!!! Dependency <NAME> was not built, because the build that makes it failed for <NAME> in this run.
      From v4.1.7. FFMPEG & FFprobe come from one build, & a build that has just failed is not run a second time.
      Correct whatever stopped the first build & run the install again.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-40000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Cannot purge sensitive memory for the <FUNCTION_NAME> function! Memory protection is Enabled. Execution cannot continue!

FILE
  Resources/Kernel/kernel.php

FATAL
  YES

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  A sensitive variable could not be destroyed & could not be released by the fallback either.
  This error only occurs if --Enable Memory Protection-- is set to TRUE in config.php.
  If --Enable Memory Protection-- is set to FALSE the same condition produces a non-fatal warning.
  <FUNCTION_NAME> names the function that owned the variable, not the function that reported it.
  A preceding WARNING!!! naming the same function identifies the variable type responsible.
  The named function submitted a variable that survived being assigned NULL.
  This error is in place to maintain syntactical integrity of the codebase.
  Logically this error should never be triggered, because assigning NULL to a variable cannot fail.
  This error is written to the log, then printed to the page, then the connection is closed.
  It is passed to errorEntry with a FALSE fatal flag so that control returns for the print & the
  close. Execution is halted immediately afterwards, so the error is fatal despite that flag.
  If this error occurs before the logging environment exists it is written to the server error log.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-41000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: mat2 is missing, unidentifiable, or too old, so no metadata was stripped! It is <STATUS>: <REASON>

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Metadata/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The Metadata pipeline strips metadata with mat2, & the manifest could not verify it.
  From v4.2.0 the error names the status the Dependency Core gave mat2 & the line of its output that says the most.
  Where mat2 gave no output to name, as when it is absent, the error ends It is <STATUS>. Where the Dependency Core could not be asked, it ends at stripped!
  absent means mat2 is not installed, outdated means it is older than 0.13, & unknown-version means it would not say which it is.
  mat2 0.13 is the floor. Run  sudo php convertCore.php --setup --install-depends --subsystem=Metadata  to install it.
  mat2 is started by the Python 3 the distribution built it for, from v4.2.0, so a host that points python3 at another runs it too.
  An unknown-version that names a module, such as cairo._cairo, means that Python lacks the module. Install mat2 again with the command above.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-41006, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so metadata cannot be stripped in isolation!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Metadata/pipeline.php

  NO

  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  mat2 runs in the sandbox, & so does FFMPEG, which strips a video, a WAV & an AIFF, & the sandbox could not be built.
  From v4.3.1 the mat2 & ffmpeg sandbox profiles both set RequireSandbox, so --Require Sandbox-- & --Require Sandbox On
  Docker-- do not decide this, & neither tool is run without a sandbox, wherever it runs. Until v4.3.1 mat2 ran without one
  where the settings allowed, & ran FFMPEG on a video, a WAV or an AIFF without one too, though the ffmpeg profile was never run
  without one.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-41007, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Could not strip the metadata from <FILE_NAME>! <TOOL> said: <MESSAGE>

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Metadata/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  The tool ran & wrote no clean copy. From v4.2.0 the error names the last line it printed, which is its reason, or ends <TOOL> said nothing.
  <TOOL> is mat2, or FFMPEG for a video, a WAV or an AIFF from v4.3.1. With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, everything it printed is
  logged before this error. FFMPEG reads an mp4 only as an MP4, an avi only as an AVI, a wmv only as an ASF, a wav only as a WAV & an
  aiff only as an AIFF, so a file that is another format than its extension says, such as a manifest given a video's name, is refused here.
  The file may be damaged, or not be the format its extension says it is.
  mat2 refuses an archive, such as an EPUB or an office document, holding a part it does not know, & names the part.
  mat2 fails when it tries to start a sandbox of its own inside this one. The Metadata pipeline keeps it from doing so for every release it knows.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-41009, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: No command could strip the metadata of <FILE_NAME>, because FFMPEG is missing or no demuxer it may use reads a <EXTENSION> file!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Metadata/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  From v4.3.1. FFMPEG strips the metadata of a video, a WAV & an AIFF, & the Metadata pipeline could build no command for one.
  FFMPEG reads each only with the demuxer its extension names, mov for an mp4, avi for an avi, asf for a wmv, wav for a wav & aiff
  for an aiff, & only where Resources/Pipelines/Shared/ffmpegDemuxers.php names that demuxer too. A module that leaves one out refuses
  every file of that kind.
  FFMPEG is checked before each file is read, & error 41002 says it is missing, so this is seen where it went missing between them.
  Restore the pipeline & its shared module from the release this installation came from.

  YES

  From v4.2.0. --Enable Authentication-- is TRUE & --Allow Anonymous Visitors-- is FALSE, so every visitor must sign in first.
  Accounts or Users in the data location is missing, is a link or cannot be written by the web server account, or the accounts
  salt in Accounts could not be read or made, so nobody can sign in.
  The visitor is shown the sign in page, which says signing in is unavailable, & nothing they send is accepted.
  -fp makes both folders & gives them to the web server account. Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp .
  It is raised for every request that arrives while it is so. Where anonymous visitors are allowed nothing is raised, every
  visitor is served anonymously, & the sign in page says signing in is unavailable.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-43001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The folder for the files of <USERNAME> could not be made in <FOLDER_PATH>, so they were not signed in!

  The request is served as an anonymous visitor's, or shown the sign in page where every visitor must sign in, so nothing of the
  user's is put where another visitor could reach it. The sign in is not ended, & the next request tries again.
  Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp , which gives Users to the web server account. A full disk refuses the folder too.

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-43100, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The host's mail system refused the <PURPOSE> email for <USERNAME>!

FILE
  Resources/Engine/Cores/emailCore.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  From v4.2.0. This host has the mail program that PHP's sendmail_path names, & PHP's mail function reported failure when it
  was handed an email. <PURPOSE> is confirm or recover. The address is never logged.
  The mail transfer agent's own log says why, & is usually /var/log/mail.log. A mail program that exits with an error for every
  message, such as one with no relay configured, is the usual cause.
  Registering tells the user their code could not be sent. Sending a code again & recovering an account answer as they always
  do, so nobody learns whether an account exists.

ERROR NUMBER RESERVATION
  43100 through 43119 are reserved for the Email Core, & 43101 through 43119 are currently unused.
  43100 is raised by the Email Core, & is documented above.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WARNINGS ABOUT ACCOUNTS
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  Every one of these is from v4.2.0. An address is never written to a log. A username is, because an administrator needs it.
  The Authentication Core raises these, from Resources/Engine/Cores/authenticationCore.php.
    WARNING!!! The accounts salt at <FILE_PATH> could not be read or made, so no account can be found.
      The salt names every record in Accounts, so without it no account can be found & nobody can sign in. It is made once, by
      the first request that needs it. Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp , which gives Accounts to the web server account. A
      salt that was changed by hand cannot be put right, because every record is named by the old one. Restore it from a backup.
    WARNING!!! Attempts of the kind <KIND> reached the limit of <LIMIT> in <SECONDS> seconds, & are refused until the oldest is that old.
      <KIND> is signin, register, code, resend or recover. code counts wrong confirmation & recovery codes. Each kind is counted
      for each address a request comes from, & signin for each account as well, so one account's password cannot be guessed from
      many addresses at once. The limit is --Sign In Attempt Limit-- & the window --Sign In Attempt Window--.
      A visitor who reached it is told there were too many attempts. Many of these in a short time is somebody guessing.
    WARNING!!! The <PURPOSE> email for <USERNAME> was not sent, because the Email Core is not loaded. Confirming an address & recovering an account are impossible until it is restored.
      The Email Core is missing or does not report the version this application pins. Signing in with a correct password still
      works. Run  php convertCore.php -v  & read the Email Core line, then restore Resources/Engine/Cores/emailCore.php from the
      matching release. --config names it as a setting that contradicts another.
    WARNING!!! An incorrect password was entered for <USERNAME>. The account cannot be recovered, because the Email Core is not loaded.
      Raised for every incorrect password while the Email Core is not loaded, because a user who forgot theirs has no way back
      in until it is restored. Where the Email Core is loaded, an incorrect password is logged as ordinary activity.
    WARNING!!! An account could not be registered for <USERNAME>, because the accounts folder could not be written.
      The visitor is told accounts are unavailable. Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp , which gives Accounts to the web server
      account. A full disk refuses the account too.
    WARNING!!! The account <USERNAME> was given a new password with a recovery code, & every sign in made before it was ended.
      The owner of the account's address asked for a recovery code & used it. Where the owner did not, somebody else reads that
      address. Disable the account with  sudo php convertCore.php --account --disable <USERNAME>  until it is settled.
    WARNING!!! A spent sign in key for <USERNAME> was presented to be exchanged again, so the sign in was ended. Somebody else may have held a copy of the key.
      A page trades its key for a new one on a timer, & a key is traded once. A spent key traded again means two pages held
      the same key, so the sign in is ended for both & the user signs in again. A browser that copies a page's key into a second
      tab does it too, & that ends the sign in & nothing more.
    WARNING!!! A workflow worker was handed an account that is gone or disabled or has no folder, so it acts for nobody.
      A signed in user started a workflow, & before its worker began the account was removed or disabled, or its folder in
      Users could not be made. The worker finds no job & stops, & the workflow does not run. Nothing was wrong with this server
      unless the folder could not be made, which -fp corrects.
    WARNING!!! How long <USERNAME> keeps their files could not be saved, because their account could not be written.
      From v4.2.3. A signed in user chose how long their files are kept, & their account could not be changed. They are told it
      could not be saved, & their files are kept as long as before. Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp , which gives Accounts to
      the web server account. A full disk refuses it too.
    WARNING!!! This page was sent without Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy, because something was written before its headers. A page of another site could reach the key a signed in tab holds.
      From v4.2.1. While accounts are on, every response carries the policy, so a page of another site can neither name a
      tab with a key of its own nor hold a tab & read its key. Something was printed before the Engine judged the request,
      which is usually a PHP notice shown because display_errors is on. Turn display_errors off, & put right what it printed.
  The Email Core raises these, from Resources/Engine/Cores/emailCore.php.
    WARNING!!! The email sender <ADDRESS> set by --Email Sender-- cannot be used, so the host's mail system names the sender.
      --Email Sender-- is not one plain address. The email is still sent, from whatever address the mail system uses.
    WARNING!!! The <PURPOSE> email for <USERNAME> was not sent. <REASON>
      <REASON> is one of these.
        PHP was built without its mail function.
        PHP names no mail program in sendmail_path.
        The mail program <PROGRAM> that PHP names in sendmail_path is not installed. Install a mail transfer agent that provides it.
        The address of <USERNAME> cannot be used.
        There is no email for <PURPOSE>.
      Nothing was handed to the host's mail system, so nobody receives the code. The first three are this host's, & --config
      names them as a setting that contradicts another while accounts are on. A user who registered is told their code could
      not be sent, & an administrator confirms the account with  sudo php convertCore.php --account --confirm <USERNAME> .
  The Authentication Manager raises these, from Resources/Engine/Managers/authenticationManager.php.
    WARNING!!! The files of <USERNAME> could not be measured against --User Storage Quota--, so a write of <BYTES> byte(s) was allowed.
      The user's folder could not be read to its end, or holds more than 200,000 entries. The write is allowed, as one to a disk
      that cannot be measured is.
    WARNING!!! An account sweep did not finish, & what it left is swept next time.
      A record that was over could not be removed, usually because a request held it at that moment. Many of these in a row mean
      Accounts cannot be written. Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp .
    WARNING!!! <COUNT> file(s) <USERNAME> kept longer than they chose to keep them could not be removed.
      From v4.2.3. The user chose how long their files are kept, & a file in their folder in Users went unchanged for longer & could
      not be removed. It is tried again in every sweep. A file the web server account does not own, or one made immutable, cannot be
      removed by it. Run  sudo php convertCore.php -fp , which gives the folder back to that account.
    WARNING!!! Command line invocation. The account <USERNAME> was changed by an administrator: <CHANGE>.
    WARNING!!! Command line invocation. The account <USERNAME> was added by an administrator.
    WARNING!!! Command line invocation. The account <USERNAME> & its files were removed by an administrator.
      A record of what --account changed, kept where the web server logs, beside the sign ins it changed. <CHANGE> is confirm,
      disable, enable, admin, user or password. Nothing was wrong.
  The Engine raises this, from Resources/Engine/engine.php.
    WARNING!!! A write was charged to a signed in user & their quota could not be read, because accounts are off or the Authentication Manager is unavailable. It was allowed.
      The listener was asked to keep a user's quota & cannot. Run  php convertCore.php -v  & read the Authentication Manager
      line, or turn --Enable Authentication-- back on.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-44000, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The data converter produced no file after <ADDITIONAL_DATA> attempt(s)!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Data/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  From v4.2.5. Dasel ran & left no file. From v4.3.1 it is asked again only where a signal stopped it,
  as many times as --File Operation Retry Count-- allows. Until then it was asked that many times whatever happened.
  A file Dasel cannot read as the format its name says, such as broken JSON or a CSV file whose rows do not line up, is the usual cause.
  With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the entry above this one carries what Dasel said, which names the line it stopped at.
  A converter its memory ceiling killed ends the same way. --Maximum Per Conversion Resources-- in config.php sets the most each type may be given,
  & a type it does not name, such as Data, is given its default.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-44001, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: The installed Dasel version is missing, unidentifiable, or too old! It is <STATUS>: <DETAIL>

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Data/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  From v4.2.5. Dasel reads every data format, so nothing is converted without it.
  <STATUS> & <DETAIL> are what  sudo php convertCore.php --setup --check-depends  says of Dasel, & are left out where there is nothing to say.
  No Debian or Ubuntu LTS release carries Dasel 3, so the Structured Data subsystem installs this application's own copy, the release binary
  Dasel publishes, pinned by version & checksum. Run  sudo php convertCore.php --setup --install-depends --subsystem="Structured Data" .
  Dasel 2, which those releases carry, is refused, because it writes XML, CSV & TOML wrongly without saying so.
  A 32 bit x86 host has no own copy, & converts no data files.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-44002, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: Bubblewrap is missing or non functional, so this data conversion cannot be isolated!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Data/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  From v4.2.5. A data conversion was refused because the server could not build a sandbox for it.
  Dasel & yq parse what a stranger uploaded, & a parser is a larger attack surface than it appears. Neither offers a native restriction
  on what it may read, so the sandbox is the only boundary available, & the conversion is refused rather than run without one.
  See error 8002 for the full list of reasons a sandbox may be unavailable & how to fix it.
  Run  php convertCore.php -v  to confirm whether this server has a working sandbox.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ERROR MESSAGE
  ERROR!!! <TIMESTAMP>, <APPLICATION_NAME>-44003, <SESSION_IDENTIFIER>: <FORMAT> needs <SHAPE> at the top of a document, & <FILE_NAME> holds <WHAT> there, so it cannot be written as <FORMAT>!

FILE
  Resources/Pipelines/Data/pipeline.php

FATAL
  NO

LOGGED
  YES

ERROR DESCRIPTION
  From v4.2.5. The document is valid, & its shape is one the format asked for cannot hold, so nothing was written rather than something wrong.
  TOML needs a table at the top of a document, & CSV needs a list of records with the same plain fields. <WHAT> is a list, a table or a
  single value. Dasel is asked what the document holds before it is converted, because Dasel 3 writes some of these with no error.
  Convert the document to JSON or YAML, which hold any shape, or change its shape first.

  says  LaTeX Error: File `<NAME>.sty' not found. , which the LaTeX subsystem, or the TeX Live package that holds it, puts right.
  A document TeX stops on is not typeset again, because TeX would stop at the same line, & a PDF TeX had begun is removed.
  A file opened by an absolute path, a hidden file or a path above the document is refused by design, & says the file was not found.
  No pages of output.  means the document typeset to nothing.

  sandbox, the host's ImageMagick policy decided too, & from v4.3.0 build-imagemagick-v7.sh installs ImageMagick's secure policy, which
  reads no image over 8000 pixels wide or high or 32 frames long.

  A file that is not an Outlook message, or one too damaged to read, is the usual cause. A file msgconvert stopped part of the way through is
  removed. With --Enhanced Logging Verbosity-- on, the entry above this one carries what msgconvert said.
  msgconvert also says it could not find the server's full name, which the sandbox does not have, & that changes nothing it writes.

WARNINGS ABOUT THE SANDBOX & REFUSED FORMATS
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  The Engine raises these, from sandboxCommand() in Resources/Engine/engine.php. Until v4.2.7 none of them was documented here.
    WARNING!!! Bubblewrap is unavailable & the <PROFILE> sandbox profile is never run without it, so a conversion was refused. Its tool reads files an upload names, & only a sandbox stops that.
      From v4.2.7. The profile sets RequireSandbox, because its tool follows a path an upload names & nothing passed with its command
      stops it, so --Require Sandbox-- & --Require Sandbox On Docker-- do not decide it. The libreoffice, calibre, inkscape, resvg, dia,
      meshlab & ffmpeg profiles set it, & -v names them beside Sandbox required. The error of the pipeline that asked follows.
      From v4.3.1 the imagemagick, tesseract & mat2 profiles set it too, though a policy or a command stops what each would read,
      because ImageMagick's & Tesseract's approval records ask for a sandbox always. The warning's last sentence is the Engine's,
      & gives the reason the first seven were traced for.
    WARNING!!! Bubblewrap is unavailable & sandboxing is required, so a conversion was refused. Install bubblewrap, or set <SETTING> to FALSE in config.php to run conversions unprotected.
      No sandbox could be built & config.php requires one, so the conversion was not run. <SETTING> is $RequireSandbox, or
      $RequireSandboxOnDocker in a container. Setting it to FALSE runs every conversion unprotected but those whose profile sets
      RequireSandbox, & is not advised. The error of the pipeline that asked follows.
    WARNING!!! Bubblewrap is unavailable & sandboxing is not required, so a conversion will run unprotected.
      No sandbox could be built, & the conversion ran without one, as config.php allows. --Throw Sandbox Warning-- set to FALSE
      silences this. Run -v as root & read the Sandbox line.
    WARNING!!! The Engine's own <FILES> for a sandbox could not be read from <FOLDER>, so a sandbox shows the host's in place of each.
      From v4.3.0, & from sandboxIdentityFlags(). A sandbox shows the passwd, group & machine-id in Resources/Engine/Sandbox in place of
      the host's, & runs its tool as the account they name. <FILES> names those missing or unreadable, & the host's is shown in place
      of each, as before v4.3.0, so the conversion still runs. Restore the folder from the release this installation came from. -v's
      Sandbox identity line says what a tool sees.
  The Stream pipeline raises this, from Resources/Pipelines/Stream/pipeline.php.
    WARNING!!! The stream converter was not launched, because no sandbox could be built.
      The sandbox refused the stream, & 21000 follows. From v4.2.7 the ffmpeg sandbox profile sets RequireSandbox, so a stream is
      refused wherever no sandbox can be built.
  The core raises these, from withoutRefusedFormats() in convertCore.php.
    WARNING!!! --<SETTING>-- in config.php lists <FORMAT>, which this application refuses to handle, so it is not offered. Take it out of the setting.
    WARNING!!! --<SETTING>-- in config.php lists <FORMATS>, which this application refuses to handle, so they are not offered. Take them out of the setting.
      From v4.2.7. A format list names an extension the core refuses, as it refuses php & html, so no button offers it & an upload of
      it is refused. recipe & opf were e-book inputs until v4.2.7, so a config.php written before then lists both in
      --Supported Ebook Input Formats--. Every page writes this until the setting is corrected. --config --repair never changes a

WARNINGS ABOUT A TIMER OF AN APPLICATION'S OWN
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  Every one of these is from v4.3.1. The Setup Core raises them, from Resources/Engine/Cores/setupCore.php, for a command an application
  puts on a schedule through $EngineTimerProvider. ENGINE_RECONCILIATION_HRPROTECT.txt enters them under Change 10.
  --setup --install-service, --setup --install-complete & -fp are the commands that raise them, & each prints what it logged.
    WARNING!!! The calendar of the timer <TIMER_NAME>, <CALENDAR>, is not one systemd reads, so the timer runs daily.
      The calendar a timer was given holds a character a calendar is not made of, or systemd-analyze did not read it.
      The timer is written with daily in its place, so the command still runs.
      systemd-analyze calendar  followed by the calendar says what systemd makes of one. Correct it, run  -fp  & restart the timer.
    WARNING!!! The timer <TIMER_NAME> was refused, because <ADDITIONAL_DATA>.
      A record the application's timer provider returned is not fit to be written into a unit root runs. What follows says which field.
      No unit is written for it. A name is lower case letters, digits & dashes, & is neither environment nor listener.
      An Argument is one word beginning --app-, a Reach is confined or host, & a Success is eight exit statuses at most.
      It is a fault in the application & not in what an administrator configured.
    WARNING!!! A timer provider named <FUNCTION_NAME> was declared & does not exist. The application puts nothing on a schedule.
    WARNING!!! The timer provider named <FUNCTION_NAME> answered with no list. The application puts nothing on a schedule.
      The contract names a function in $EngineTimerProvider that is not defined, or that returned something other than a list of timers.
      No unit is written. It is a fault in the application.
    WARNING!!! An application asks for sixteen timers at most, so every one after the sixteenth was refused.
      The provider returned more than sixteen. The first sixteen were examined, & the rest have no unit.
    WARNING!!! The timer <TIMER_NAME> was not built, because <ADDITIONAL_DATA>.
      A unit runs PHP & the application by their whole paths, & one of the two holds something other than letters, digits, dots, dashes,
      underscores & slashes. A unit splits a line at a space & reads a percent sign as its own, so such a path is not written into one.
      What follows names the path. Install the application in a folder whose path holds none of those.
    WARNING!!! The timer <TIMER_NAME> could not be written to <FILE_PATH>. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
      A service or a timer could not be written into /etc/systemd/system, & what follows says why. It is usually a full disk,
      or a folder on the way there that an account other than root could replace. Nothing was enabled, & --setup --install-service exits 1.
      A unit that is a link is left as it is, & so is the unit beside it, because an administrator masks a unit by putting a link where it was.
      sudo systemctl unmask  takes a mask away, & the command can then be run again.
    WARNING!!! The timers of <APPLICATION_NAME> can only be written while running as root.
      A command that writes a unit was reached by an account that is not root. Nothing was written. Run it with sudo.
    WARNING!!! The timers of <APPLICATION_NAME> were written & systemd could not be told to read them again. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
      The units are in place & systemctl daemon-reload failed. What follows is what it said.
      systemd goes on with what it had read. Run  sudo systemctl daemon-reload  & read what it answers.

WARNINGS ABOUT THE EVENT RECORD
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  Both are the Engine's from v4.3.1, & were HRProtect's from its v0.0.3. Neither changes what a command does or the status it exits
  with, & each is said once for a command.
  The Engine raises these, from Resources/Engine/engine.php.
    WARNING!!! The event record is neither written to nor read by this command, because the Event Core is <STATE>. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
      A command had an event to keep, or was asked to read the record, & the Event Core could not be used.
      The state is detached, refused or unable, & what follows it says why.
      detached means Resources/Engine/Cores/eventCore.php is not installed.
      refused means it reports no version or one the application does not pin, or that it could not be loaded.
      unable means the command is not root's, or that the Events folder is not root's alone or could not be made.
      Run  sudo php <entry point> -fp  for a folder that is not root's alone. Only a command that runs as root keeps an event.
      The command does its work & reports it as it would have.
      php <entry point> -v  says where the Event Core stands. A record switched off with --Record Events-- raises no warning, because
      that was chosen.
    WARNING!!! An event could not be written to the event record, & no other that cannot be is said by this command. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
      The Event Core was ready & one event was not kept. What follows is the reason the Event Core gave.
      A full disk is the usual reason, & the part of a line it let through was taken back out.
      A file of the record that is not root's alone, that is a link or that has a second name is refused, & is never written through.
      An event that is not well formed is refused as well. That is a fault in the application that kept it & is worth reporting.
      The command goes on, & what it found is in its report & its log. Every later event this command cannot keep is passed over
      without a word.

WARNINGS ABOUT AN ENGINE THAT DID NOT START
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.

    WARNING!!! The Engine did not start, because its Environment Core, Resources/Engine/Cores/environmentCore.php, was refused. <ADDITIONAL_DATA> Put it back from the Engine's release.
      From v4.3.1. The Engine pins its Environment Core exactly, in getAcceptedEnvironmentCore(), & does not start without it, because
      nearly everything the Engine does calls into it. What follows says whether it is missing, reports no version, reports another version
      or could not be loaded. Until v4.3.1 a missing one was a warning that said an application could supply its own definitions, & the
      first command that needed one, -v & -h among them, ended with a PHP fatal error.
    WARNING!!! The Engine is unavailable. Dependency location, startup keys, environment validation & per operation resource limits are unavailable until it is repaired.
      The Engine is missing, is not the version the application pins, or loaded & did not start. A warning before this one says which.
      -v & -h are answered by the kernel, which says what it can. A web request is halted with error 28005, & every other command is refused.
      Put back Resources/Engine from the release the application came from, into the installation, & run -v again.
    WARNING!!! The command <COMMAND> was refused because the Engine is unavailable.
      Every command but -v, -h & --app needs the Engine. The command exits 1 & says what to run. Where the installation is in
      Application in the data location & the command was run from another folder, it names the installation, because a drop-in
      unpacked into the folder the web server serves rather than into Application does this.

WARNINGS ABOUT WHAT ROOT TRUSTS
  A warning carries no error number & never halts anything. Each says what happened & what was done about it.
  The kernel raises these, from Resources/Kernel/kernel.php. Root runs, reads & writes nothing another account could change, & -fp
  gives each location root manages back to root. ABOUT_MANAGED_LOCATIONS says what each location must be.
    WARNING!!! Root is running code an account other than root could have changed, because <COMMAND> is what gives the installation at <FOLDER> back to root. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
      Every other command run as root halts with 28008 on such a tree. -fp, --setup & --config --repair are what give a tree to root,
      & a fresh clone or an older installation is not root's until one of them has run, so they still run. What follows names the
      first thing that failed, & the same is printed. An account that could change the tree could have changed this command too.
    WARNING!!! Root corrects nothing beneath <FOLDERS>, because another account can change them.
      A folder above the hosted location, above HRProprietary or above an additional data location is not root's alone. Root goes on,
      & leaves everything beneath each such folder as it is. The report printed with it says what each folder needs. A folder above
      the data location halts with 31024 instead.
    WARNING!!! A folder above the data location <FOLDER> could not be examined.
    WARNING!!! A folder above the hosted location or an additional data location could not be examined.
      Root judges every folder above those locations before it touches anything below them, & one could not be read. Root goes on with
      the folders it could judge. A folder that vanished while it was read does this, & the next command judges it again.
    WARNING!!! The data location at <FOLDER> could be changed by another account, so root took it back, root:root 0755, as -fp does. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
    WARNING!!! The data location at <FOLDER> could be changed by another account & could not be given back to root, so the install secret below it is out of root's reach. <ADDITIONAL_DATA> <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
      Root reads & writes the install secret only where no account but root & the web server account could replace it. A repair, &
      every command run as root once Secrets is in the data location, takes the data location back first, because -fp could not give
      it its role otherwise. What follows says what was wrong, & in the second why it could not be changed. An old chown -R, a restored
      backup or a bind mount leaves one so. Only a copy of the secret root owns counts for the rest of the command.
    WARNING!!! The data location <FOLDER> does not exist & was not created, because it is beneath a system folder, a folder another account can change or a link. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
      Root creates a missing data location only beneath a folder it can trust, & never beneath a system folder such as /etc or /usr.
      Make it by hand, or set --Data Storage Directory-- in config.php to a folder that is not beneath one, & run -fp as root.
    WARNING!!! The data location <FOLDER> was created & could not be given to root. <ADDITIONAL_DATA>
    WARNING!!! The data location did not exist & was created at <FOLDER>.
      The data location was missing, so root made it, because the install secret is kept in it. The first comes before the second
      where its owner could not be changed, & -fp gives it back to root.
    WARNING!!! <FOLDER> held <NUMBER> folder(s) an account other than root could change. Each was made root's alone before anything in it was removed.
      Root removed a tree, such as an old build of a dependency, & folders in it were not root's alone. Each was made so before it was
      emptied, so nothing could swap one for a link while root worked in it. The tree is gone. That another account could change part
      of it is worth knowing.
